## 2026-11-13 NETWORK NEVER CHANGES A READING; MODEL TIMEOUTS VISIBLE (worker)
- **rd10 "show Pariss on map" failure.** It does not reproduce on this tree: alone, with `PSYS2_SUITE=full`, and on one core with 8 busy loops (first test 11.9 s, still the right reading). The name repair path contains no fetch at all (nameCandidates, parse; no catalog is attached in Node), so 6 s was not a fetch: it was the wall-clock budgets (name budget 30/120 ms, leniency 3 s, `guard.soft()`) that the SPEED-INDEPENDENT commit already replaced by step budgets; the reviewer's runs were before it. The real network defect found instead: a lookup (`preloadThings`, `knowledgeAnswer`, `knowledgeFallback`) awaited the catalog without any bound, so a hanging fetch held the turn until the watchdog, and an unreachable catalog turned "what is a comet" into "I don't know what a comet is" (replacing the reading). Fixed: `within()` bound (engine.knowledgeDeadlineMs, default 6000), an unreachable catalog changes nothing, `createKnowledgeSource` fetch aborts after 6 s, stats in `engine.knowledgeStats`.
- Rule A test `tests/network-never-changes-reading.test.mjs`: no catalog vs every fetch rejected vs every fetch hanging; segments identical, turn settles (no wall-clock assertion).
- Rule B: `scripts/lib/no-network.mjs` preload (fetch and http(s) to non-loopback throw and are logged; `run-tests.mjs` fails the run on any logged attempt). The first full run with the guard logged 0 attempts (existing tests already stub fetch); self-test inside the Rule A file.
- MODEL-TIMEOUTS (spec 06): `_noteModelTimeout`, `engine.onModelTimeout`, `trace.formalizer.modelTimeouts` / `modelTimeoutCount`, `engine.modelStats`, normalizer via `guard.modelTimeout`; webchat debug panel banner once + count + toast (`debug.noteModelTimeout`). Deadlines unchanged. `tests/model-timeouts.test.mjs`. Both new files are in SMOKE.
- Phone-profile rate: NOT measured. | Reading | Rephrase what I cannot read | `true` | Operational English set: 98.6% right and 0.4% wrong with the dispatcher and the normalizer, against 95.4% and 0.5% with the rules alone (training/RESULTS.md). |
| Reading | Do what I understood and say what I ignored | `true` | In the shipped pipeline since 7.10; evaluations count a partly understood message as right only when the executed part is right. |
| Reading | Guess loose words and say how I took them | `true` | In the shipped pipeline since 7.15: a guess is acceptable because the worlds are harmless and the reply says how it was read. |
| Reading | Answer at once when the rules read everything | `true` | Passed its gate: model ready under 5 s and median answer 300 ms or less at 4x CPU throttle; evaluation unchanged (0 differences). |
| Languages and voice | Language | `en` | English is the language of the worlds; the browser language is used once, at the first start. |
| Languages and voice | How messages are translated | `auto` | Owner rule 2026-11-12: with a browser Translator available it is the translator; the rules are the fallback. |
| Languages and voice | Suggest switching language | `auto` | Selected language is the only language; the hint is a suggestion only (LANG-FIXED). |
| Languages and voice | English accent | `en-US` | US English is the default of most voices. |
| Languages and voice | Voice | `` | Automatic picks the best installed voice of the language. |
| Languages and voice | Speech speed | `1` | Normal speed. |
| Languages and voice | Keep listening until I say stop | `true` | Hands-free is the point of the microphone button. |
| Languages and voice | Only respond when I address you | `false` | Off: a typed message and a clear command always pass; it is for noisy rooms only. |
| Languages and voice | Read answers aloud | `false` | Off: sound should never start by surprise. |
| Privacy and history | Keep a history of my messages | `true` | Real phrasings are the training data we need (industrial research principle); nothing is sent. |
| Privacy and history | Include button presses in exports | `false` | Buttons are not human phrasing. |
| Developer | Show how each answer was made | `false` | A diagnostic, not for everyday use. |
| Developer | Developer mode | `false` | A diagnostic. |
| Developer | Show timing of the last message | `false` | A diagnostic. |
| Developer | Engine for shop, arithmetic and task worlds | `matrix` | User decision 2026-11-11: matrix is the default. |
| Developer | Check readings against what the worlds contain | `veto` | User decision 2026-11-12: veto is the default. |
| Developer | Time limits | `built-in` | Defaults come from the compute-budget work (src/dialog/time-limits.mjs). |

Talk card with no training (87 messages whose world is talk): A 0 (no label), B 21, C 4 (the prior points to trained worlds). Cost of the card on messages of other worlds (right with/without): B 1481/1491 dialogues, 692/699 operational; C none or 1.

Decision rule: NOT met. C is not at least as good as A everywhere (operational 93.9 vs 95.3/96.1, wrong-tab-ops 84.5 vs 87.0 against A1, knowledge 54.2 vs 55.3; it is better on dialogues, wrong-tab, minimal pairs against A) and B loses more than 3 points on 3 of 4 hold-outs. Findings: (1) the cards are generated from patterns, so B depends on their coverage (arith "less than", "split by", greetings, typos are not on any card; near-word repair helped some); (2) the prior weight trades the trained case against the hold-out (30 and 45 best trained, 15 best masked), so the encoder cannot be both a strong prior and harmless for a world it does not know; (3) where the symbolic rules have evidence (referent, named world, running game, last action, wrong tab) they fix what the encoder gets wrong (dialogues +14.8, wrong-tab +7.5 over A); (4) the validator in A counts "not validated" as asked, A1 is the fairer encoder baseline. Not done: end-to-end owner-log replay with B (the world-level owner set has 13 messages), masked A. sopworld gaps: questions.md Q3.

Same at 12 MB/s: worlds 1.4 s, reasoner 3.8 s, model 6.6 s, catalog 0.4 s (3.9 s through the 1.2 s/file proxy). **What to speed up:** the model (68 MB, 83 s at 1 MB/s; cached afterwards), the worlds bundle (5.3 MB JSON, `index.json` parsed twice: text then JSON.parse in the worker), the reasoner start (3.8-4.4 s at 4x CPU, after the bundle is in), the voices wait (2.5 s bounded when the browser has none; should end at the first `voiceschanged` or not wait for the synthesis support check), the 1.3 MB catalog vocabulary (could be a smaller prefix index).
Known limits: the panel's weights are only good from the second start (the first has no history); `fail` has no Retry button (the old banner had one; the reload or Settings > Models retry remain); `scripts/test-webchat-phone.mjs` selectors were moved from `.p2-mt-banner` to `.p2-progress` but not re-run (it needs the translation model files).

## 2026-11-13 (worker, ACTS x SCHEMAS A1-A4 + the proof world)
Branch commits, not pushed. Spec: `specs/21-acts-and-schemas.md` (one page + engine notes). Objections, risks and cost estimate: questions.md (written first, not waiting).
**What exists.**
- `worlds/acts/` (module world, never a routing target): `acts.mjs` (browser-safe, no imports), `lexicon.sop` (GENERATED by `scripts/build-acts-lexicon.mjs`: `acts.verb.*` seed phrase groups marked TEMPORARY for deletion when `k.actions` is read through the catalog, `acts.det`, `lex.acts.rel`).
- A1: 14 act ids (12 acts, undo/redo and save/load as pairs), six roles (thing, set, attribute, value, place, amount; set = a thing text that resolves to several: "all the tables", "tables 1 and 3"), the act grammar once as `FORMS` (about 45 forms; a world gets those whose act it declares and whose condition its schema meets: about 41 patterns for the seating plan).
- A2: schema format in SOP Lang (`schema.sop`: `acts.schema`, `acts.kind`, `acts.attr`; kinds with plural / numbered / named / max / `in` + `capacity` / `any=knowledge`, attributes with type number|enum|colour|text|bool, min, max, default, step, more/less words, synonyms), `parseSchema` with reasons for a wrong schema, `scripts/build-schema-world.mjs <dir> [--check]` generating lexicon, patterns, operations, templates and a stub `ops.mjs`; `card(schema)` (meta.worlds card words, not yet wired into build-world-cards), `examples(schema)` (help examples), `sentences(schema)` (A5 coverage sentences, act x kind).
- A3: `createExecutor(schema, hooks)`: `run(state, req)` -> `{ status, note, diff {added, removed, changed, moved, linked}, guesses }`; state is the world's `ctx.state` (items, focus, history, future, saved, log, links, groups); undo is one snapshot per request, redo; the reply and the why-answer are built from the act log and the diff ("Because you said \"make table 2 blue\": I made table 2 blue", "Table 1 is not red: its colour is blue", "Nothing I did changed that: table 2 has colour white because that is the default of a table"); limits (max per kind, capacity of a container, ranges, history and item limits) refuse and change nothing; every loop ticks the turn budget. Both reasoners: the world has no matrix.sop, so the engine runs it through the ops path in both modes (tested in both).
- A4: delegation tiers said > focus > scene > knowledge > default for thing, place, attribute, amount, with the guess said in the reply; "not here" says what the world has and, through hooks, where the thing or act is (`whereKind`, `whereAct`); otherwise ONE question naming the guessed act and thing (`pending` + `interceptInput`: the answer is the role, or yes/no for a guessed act; a new command cancels it and is read normally); `lastResort` is exported as the `outOfScope` hook of the world of the open tab (never a bare "I did not understand" there; with no tab open the engine's own fallback still speaks, see risks).
- Fixture `tests/fixtures/worlds/test.seating` (schema.sop 49 lines; rest generated; `tests.sop` 6 tests). `tests/acts.test.mjs`: 26 tests, both reasoners.
**Adapter for sopworld 0.16.0.** Not vendored in this branch. The hooks are the thin adapter: `hooks.focus(state)` (std.focus), `hooks.know` (kind synonyms and stand-in, usual place, attribute defaults, colour words: the generic catalog reading of agent (a)), `hooks.whereKind` / `whereAct` (meta.worlds cards), `hooks.send`, and `hooks.layout` is reserved for the `place` act with the SPACE world (agent b; not called yet). Nothing in acts.mjs imports the library.
**Measurement (P2 preview, not the P2 gate).** 40 natural requests written without looking at the grammar, run in both reasoners: first run 35 read, after adding general forms (declarative "table 2 should be blue", "what colour is table 2", "who sits at", attribute counts) 39 read. READ IS NOT CORRECT: reading the replies showed 9 wrong or odd answers in the first run (a table added for "table 2 to have 8 seats", "how many seats does table 1 have" counting tables, "table 1 and table 2" not coordinated, "next to" read silently as "at", duplicate names), each a general cell fixed once; the real P2 needs 60 requests judged by hand. The one unread: the fragment "a bigger table 1" (dialogue module).
**Risks and findings for the reviewer.**
1. The engine's discourse layer owns "redo" and cancels it on any later turn (a query between undo and redo ends it). The executor's own redo works only when a host calls it directly. Same for every tab world today; a candidate engine fix: do not clear the redo stack on a read-only turn.
2. The engine rewrote the pronoun in "why is it blue" to "why is blue blue" (some referent substitution before the patterns); the why-answer falls back to the focus when no thing is named. Cause not found in this session.
3. Lexicons of attribute / value words must not be `class=noun` (the engine takes the last noun for "it"); the generator uses `modifier`.
4. Each operation runs several times per turn (dry-run probes, 3 REQ lines per turn seen); the executor mutates only the real state it is given, so this is safe, but the cost per turn is x3 on state snapshots for undo (not measured; the seating plan is far below 1 ms).
5. No tab open: the engine's "did not understand" path only consults `outOfScope` for the world of the open tab, so in the tests a sentence that no form reads still gets the generic reply. A host-level fix is the same hook for the last world used.
6. Not done (declared): `card(schema)` is not wired into `scripts/build-world-cards.mjs` (the cards are generated from patterns anyway, so the seating world gets a card by the existing path); `hooks.layout` / SPACE; the coverage grid page (A5) beyond `sentences()`; P1 and P3.
**API for the pilots.** A pilot world = directory with `world.sop` (imports `world.acts`, `world.lang.common`), `legend.sop` (imports acts), `schema.sop`, `tests.sop`; run `node scripts/build-schema-world.mjs worlds/<id>`; the world's `ops.mjs` is generated and exports `executor` (call `executor.run(state, { act, ... })` or pass hooks by building your own `createExecutor(schema, hooks)` in a hand-written ops.mjs that re-exports the act functions). The renderer reads `ctx.state.items` (id, kind, n, name, attrs, place {rel, ref}), `state.links`, `state.groups`, `state.focus`; every act result carries `diff`. For avatar and diagram: add kinds with `any=knowledge` for drawable things, a `part` relation as `in=` containers, and attributes by type; P1 needs `know.kind` from facts.mjs and `k.faces`.
**Test minutes (this task):** about 4 (acts tests 8 runs of 3 s, one smoke 17 s twice, loop-guard 1 s, meta-worlds/legends/lang-common/no-romanian once). No full suite. Smoke 16.7 s, 0 fail (the first smoke found the webchat stale and one unguarded `while` in acts.mjs, both fixed).

## 2026-11-13 — avatar probe causes (offline face data, act owner, undo of questions), k.layouts in the published format

**1. Bundled default face = data.** `scripts/build-default-face.mjs` now keeps every slot (16, with the catalog's aliases: eyeglasses, specs, cap, mustache ...), EVERY preset (14: boy, man, robot ...) and every expression (angry, sad ...), the four colours, and for each slot every variant that a preset or expression names, else its first (48 kB, was 13 kB). "none" is implicit in the logic (a slot is removable; "what hats are there" says "... and none"). Regenerated from the published catalog. PROGRESS: the Avatar tab reports `faces` (start / end) and, when the catalog cannot be had, `fail('faces', "The face set could not be loaded", "the bundled face works ...")`; seen in the Chrome probe. No avatar pattern touched.
**2. The act belongs to the open tab (general rule, `_tabOwnsAct` in src/dialog/index.mjs, both reasoners).** Cause: "remove the hat" had no reading in ui.avatar (no bare "remove" pattern), so art.draw's `remove {words}` (tier 5, no competitor) answered "I can't find the hat in the picture". Rule: a reading of a world the message does not name, whose act (last word of its action) is also an action of the world on the open tab, whose dry run finds nothing to act on / refuses, and that no other reading replaces, moves to the tab world's pattern for that act, arguments by shape (one text argument to one text argument); the tab world answers in its own words. It does not fire when the foreign pattern has words of its own beside the act ("remove milk from my LIST") or a slot of its lexicon (a thing of that world is named): kids.tasks tests n8 and n55 showed that first. Result: "remove the hat" -> "I took off a bow hat.", "remove eyeglasses" -> "I have no glasses already.", "remove the unicorn" -> "I have nothing like unicorn on my face". Side effect found by test-world: a FRESH session's default tab is the Avatar ("robot"), so art.draw test err01 ("Delete the car." on an empty picture) now needs `given="ui.shell:activeTab=draw"` (done); every other world passes in both reasoners (only the known ledger row media.music.sh04 fails).
**Why invariant (c) did not catch it:** (c) asserts "the tab did not change unless a tab is named"; the wrong-world reply left the tab where it was, so it saw nothing. New invariant in tests/invariants.test.mjs (both reasoners): over the English owner-log messages plus remove/delete probes on four tabs, no refusal (`unsupported` / `missing`) comes from a world that is not the tab's, is not named in the message, for an act the tab's world has. Checked that it FAILS without the rule (5 hits on the Avatar tab) and passes with it. Wider finding for the ledger (not fixed, by the rule): the same sweep without the "act of the tab" condition shows ~40 owner-log turns answered by a refusal of another world (single letters "a", "e" on the Hangman tab answered "We are not playing hangman" by games.hangman, "why?" answered by games.guess, "draw ..." on the Charts tab by data.charts); they are one class, "wrong world answers with a refusal", for the analysis script.
**3. Undo / redo count only state-changing requests.** Cause: `commitTurn` (src/dialog/discourse.mjs) made an action of every turn whose world state differed, and a question leaves state (the avatar's offered choices), the exemption existed only for the first use of a lazy state. A turn whose frames are all questions is now never an action. "put on earrings; what hats are there?; undo; redo" -> redo says `OK, I did it again: "put on earrings"` (was: the question), both reasoners; test added (also on the draw tab).
**4. Ledger:** training/eval/failures.jsonl: "make green eyes" (act phrase), "make the avatar an angry boy" (two acts in one sentence), "add eyeglasses" and "eyeglasses" (act phrase; the avatar patterns have no bare add / bare part).
**5. The 14 turns in Chrome** (`scripts/probe-avatar-turns.mjs`, new; fixture catalog served by a local server through `knowledgeUrl`; `--offline` points it at a dead port). Same words in both, no page errors in either:

| said | catalog reachable | catalog unreachable (bundled face) |

## P3 (ACTS x SCHEMAS reader): act + roles tagger at three sizes, 2026-11-13 (worker)

Full write-up and per-role numbers: `training/results/act-tagger-p3.md` (+ `act-tagger-<size>.json`, `act-tagger-phone-<size>.json`, `act-tagger-agreement.json`). Data generator `scripts/gen-act-tagger-data.mjs` (rerunnable, discovers every schema.sop with an acts.schema wire), trainer `training/psys2_train/act_tagger.py`, phone profile `training/experiments/browser-limits/tagger-run.mjs`. Models stay in `training/runs/` (ignored); nothing is added to docs/webchat/models. The DistilBERT dispatcher is untouched.

| model | params | int8 MB | cold load at 1 MB/s | graph create at 4x | per message at 4x (median / p95, 9 tokens) | per message at 1x | seen: act / span F1 | held-out: act / span F1 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DistilBERT base (6 x 768) | 66.4 M | 67.0 | 67 s | 1.27 s | 72.5 / 97.6 ms | 17.9 ms | 99.42 / 99.82 | 99.03 / 95.38 |
| MiniLM-class 6 x 384 | 22.7 M | 23.0 | 23 s | 1.26 s | 21.1 / 28.5 ms | 4.9 ms | 99.55 / 99.74 | 99.07 / 95.13 |
| MiniLM-class 4 x 384 | 19.2 M | 19.3 | 19 s | 1.10 s | 12.6 / 17.5 ms | 3.4 ms | 99.43 / 99.74 | 98.83 / 93.96 |

Held out: diagram.sketch and ui.avatar; trained on plan.seating, test.seating and 60 synthetic schemas built from knowledge words (242,744 sentences). Phone profile = headless Chrome, ort-web 1.31, 4x CPU throttle, 300 real test sentences; the int8 act equals the fp32 CUDA act on 300/300.

Agreement with dispatcher + patterns on the operational + dialogue sets (the rules' act read by verb from the winning request action; 748 messages comparable, 443 in the doing worlds): DistilBERT 80.6 %, 6x384 76.1 %, 4x384 77.0 %; single-command messages of the doing worlds (362): 86.7 / 81.8 / 81.8 %. A lower bound: the action -> act mapping is a heuristic, the tagger sees no tab or history, and compound messages are one input.

Findings: (1) all three transfer to the unseen worlds on act (98.8 to 99.1 %) and on role spans except thing / set (held-out span F1 95.4 / 95.1 / 94.0 against 99.8 / 99.7 / 99.7 on seen worlds: gap 4.4 / 4.6 / 5.8 points, within 5 for the first two). (2) The generated sets are easy, so the table separates the sizes by cost, not accuracy: 6x384 is 2.9x smaller and 3.4x faster than DistilBERT for 0.25 points of held-out span F1; the 4-layer model 3.5x smaller and 5.8x faster for 1.4 points. (3) Cold load at 1 MB/s is what moves: 67 s against 23 s and 19 s (creating the graph at 4x is 1.1 to 1.3 s for all). (4) On real messages the tagger needs the logs: compound messages, follow-ups and a none / question class are not in the label set. Question for the reviewer: compound messages (segment first, as the dispatcher does, or tag several acts) and whether `none` is a class. No engine JS touched (scripts and training files only), so no smoke run.

## 2026-11-13 P1 round 4 (branch p1-round4): the generated avatar no longer takes other worlds' sentences
Cause: on the default tab (the Avatar) the avatar's forms are tier 3 whatever other world reads the sentence, so every form with a free slot (save / "write down", "who are you", "my name is", "is a square a rectangle", place.dir) captured sentences of kids.common, kids.shapes, docs.editor, user.profile, kids.tasks, media.music, tictactoe, art.draw: 34 failing test-world cases. General fixes: (1) the formalizer's decline gets the segment text, the tab and `claim`, and a world's catch-all reading (free text slots) is dropped when another world has a reading with no free text at all, unless the world holds the focus of the dialogue (lastWorld) or says so (`accepts` returns 'focus'); (2) tabClaim only when the sentence itself says yourself / your / the avatar / the face; (3) `save` only with a face word or "save this/it as"; place / remove / ask need every word to be a face word; (4) `who are you` is not an avatar form; (5) a fallback by `outOfScope` needs a thing of the world (an act verb alone, "Add please", is the engine's not understood). Result: `test-world all` in both reasoners 33 worlds, 0 failing cases (ui.avatar 18/18, plan.seating 7/7, art.draw 596/596 ops and 600/600 matrix); the avatar-related test files: 4 not ok left, all marked todo or the old canonical-examples (talk) failures; tests/acts, pilot-avatar, things, dialogue, bounded-captures, rd10 green; smoke 16 s. Merged main (3c4a2211, art.draw pilot).

## 2026-11-13 TRANSFER: charts and the document as views, selection phrases, make-document, timeline (T6-lite)
**What changed (general mechanisms, no code per pair).** (1) `things.show` takes a selection (`select()` in things.mjs: the discussion, what one view holds by the verb or the place, else a name/kind) and views may say `many` / `counts` / `facts`; three `signal` patterns in session.things read "a chart of what I drew / of the things in the plan / about the current discussion" into that one operation. (2) The drawing board is bridged without being a view: its referents (`ctx.sessionFacts()`, new hook next to `ctx.dialogueFocus`, also set on the dry-run fork, or every such request is judged "missing" in the dry run) enter the store. (3) `data.charts` and `docs.editor` are views through a `view` and an `adopt` operation (26 and 45 hand-written lines of ops, plus 4 operation/template entries each). (4) `doc.make`: the act make-document with a title and content in one request (18 lines, 2 patterns, test in tests.sop). (5) M1 `sessionContent` and its `factsList` / `factsProse` are deleted (about 45 lines of dialogue.mjs); the dialogue test keeps the facts check, the chart/document behaviour is in tests/things.test.mjs. (6) `worlds/plan.timeline`: a view from a schema alone (52 lines of schema.sop, everything else generated, 30 lines of tab plugin, `tabOnly=yes`), things with a temporal aspect (`k.time`) are taken as moments even when the schema has no word for their kind (adopt: a kind the world declares with an aspect the thing has).
**T1 matrix, 5 views + timeline (both reasoners identical; 3 views alone were 60 cells 22 right / 38 not applicable / 0 wrong).** 34 things (30 + 4 calendar things from Talk) made in the avatar, the seating plan and Talk, each sent to every other view: 170 cells, 89 right, 76 not applicable said plainly, 5 wrong. By target: talk 20/0/0, seating 1/23/0, avatar 1/23/0, chart 29/0/5, document 34/0/0, timeline 4/30/0 (right / not applicable / wrong). The 5 wrong are one class: "send table N to the chart" (a number in the sentence) is read by the chart's own data pattern (`r2.create.data.trail`, the named-world tier of routing) before the seating plan's send; not patched, in the ledger class below. With `--draw` (pilot art.draw as the fourth view): 264 cells, 119 / 123 / 22; the extra 17 wrong are the existing talk/avatar to draw cells (12 on main before this work) plus the 4 calendar things. T2 is unchanged (40 cells 8 right / 26 not applicable / 6 wrong, same as before). Chart and document are columns only: they publish no things of their own yet, so no row is made in them (limit in the spec).
**Hand-written lines outside schema + renderer + own rules, per world:** talk: 0 new (its send branches target draw / diagram / map, none target charts or the editor; unchanged, goes when those are views); data.charts: 26 ops + 12 sop (operations) + 8 (templates); docs.editor: 63 ops (make 16, view+adopt 47) + 19 sop (patterns) + 18 + 12; plan.timeline: 0 lines of code (schema 52, world 17, legend 6, tests 19; tab plugin 30); session.things (shared, once for all pairs): select 35 + bridge/show 48 lines, 3 patterns, 2 lexicons. Replaced per-pair code: M1 sessionContent (dialogue.mjs, about 45 lines).
**Checks.** test-world all, both reasoners: the one failure of main (media.music sh04), nothing added. Targeted: tests/things.test.mjs 11/11 (2 new), tests/dialogue.test.mjs unchanged failure (a fragment with a place, fails on main too). Smoke once: green except "the built webchat is fresh" (docs/webchat not rebuilt here: the reviewer rebuilds after merging). Found on the way and fixed as general causes: the dry run (`forkForValidation`) had no session facts, so every "chart of the discussion" read was rejected as missing; the view words must be a lexicon or "number of items on my list" is read as a request for a view (test-world caught it: kids.tasks sw122).
**Ledger classes (not patched):** a sentence naming the chart world plus a number is read as chart data over the open tab's send (named-world routing tier); `acts.mjs` generated examples of plan.timeline say "Add a table" (the generator's example text is seating-specific); a thing's time is not read from `k.time` (`when` defaults to 1).
**Test minutes:** about 12 (T1 runs 10 x 6 s, test-world all 3 x 70 s, smoke 4 x 20 s, targeted tests 3 s). No full suite.

## 2026-11-13 TRANSFER: the map as a view, a send that names a view, T3 rule transfer (common sense at the knowledge tier)
**What changed (general mechanisms, no code per pair).** (1) `geo.map` is a view of the session things (`geo.map.view` / `.adopt`, geographic aspect, many): pins for what the map can place (name, kind, or `lat` / `lon`), plain words for the rest; selection phrases "show it on the map" and "put the places we talked about on the map" (patterns of `session.things`; the noun names an aspect: `ASPECT_NOUN_TEMPORARY`); Talk's per-pair `send` branch to the map, its pattern `pat.talk.send.map` and the phrase `lang.talk.tomap` are deleted (the map is reached by `things.show`). (2) The T1 class "send table 1 to the chart": a rule in `src/formalizer/rules/index.mjs` next to the decline loop: a free-text reading whose wildcard slot starts with the verb of an act that another world's act pattern reads in the same sentence yields to the act (the act pattern names `$acts.verb.<act>` and its action ends with the act; nothing is listed per phrase). (3) T3: `src/knowledge/commonsense.mjs` is the one lookup of where a kind usually is (`k.scenes` place, `k.commonsense` at, taught or catalog, the kind-of chain); Talk's answer uses it for every kind (it was only for taught ones), the drawing board asks it for a kind with no row of its own (default place fallback, and the advice in both reasoners through `ctx.rulesAdvise`, a flag added to the operation context so a kind is never advised twice), `parseTeach` reads "a house stands on the ground" and the other habitat verbs. `scripts/exp-transfer.mjs` got T3 (`--only t3`, `--ledger` appends the wrong cells to `training/eval/failures.jsonl`) and a map column in T1.
**Measures (both reasoners identical unless said).** T3, 20 facts x drawing / diagram / Talk = 60 cells: before 16 right / 14 na / 30 wrong, after 27 / 14 / 19. Talk 9 -> 19 of 20; drawing 7 -> 8 right of 15 that apply (the real board, no pilot); diagram 0 of 11. T1 with the map as a seventh column: 204 cells, 97 right, 107 not applicable said plainly, 0 wrong (the 5 wrong chart cells of the last entry are the send class of (2)); map column 3 right (Paris, France, Japan from Talk) / 31 na. T2 unchanged against HEAD (8 right / 23 na / 9 wrong in the 40 other-view cells with the pilot board on the path; I ran HEAD in a scratch copy to check; the 9 are the pilot drawing taking "put on a cottage" and "seat the dogs", and the ones already in the last entry).
**T3 ledger (38 rows: 19 per reasoner, `cause` field), by cause.** diagram has no place semantics, nothing asks the knowledge tier (11 per reasoner): a connection is an arrow with no meaning, so there is nothing to refuse or label; the fix is a view with a spatial / containment relation, not a patch; the drawing grammar does not read "<kind> in the <place>" for a kind of the catalog or a taught kind (4: lighthouse, snowman, train, plane: "I did not understand ... Draw the sky"); "in the water" is read as the bottom band and the place word is lost (3: sun, cloud, car); another world (kids.animals) reads "ducks float" first and keeps the fact private (1). Facts about abilities (birds can fly, a dog can bark, ducks float) have no place, so the drawing and the diagram say plainly they have no use for them (na).
**Honest notes.** (a) 10 of the 20 facts are "taught" but 3 of those kinds (snowman, train, lighthouse) are also in the fixture catalog (`k.scenes`), so those cells would be right from the catalog alone; the pure taught cases that differ from the catalog are the villa / cottage / yacht / taxi kind-of facts and "ducks float" and "penguins cannot fly". (b) `things.show` with nothing to show now answers `unsupported` (was `missing`) because "put it in the doc" on the chart tab is a refusal, and a chart test (r2132) expects it. (c) The map's "show it on the map" in the Talk tab still goes through Talk's `send.view` pattern (tabClaim) into `things.show`; from any other tab the new `select.it` reads it; "where is it on the map" is not read (a ledger class). (d) The webchat build was not rebuilt here.
**Test minutes:** about 14 (T1 4 x 14 s, T2 5 x 12 s, T3 8 x 9 s, test-world all 4 x 65 s, targeted tests 3 x 12 s). No full suite, no smoke (once at the end, see below).

## 2026-11-13 KNOWLEDGE-OUT steps 1-3, 4a (colours and number words), measured
**What changed (general mechanisms, no code per phrase).** (1) `scripts/knowledge-inventory.mjs` lists every kb fact type, code table (>= 10 words) and lexicon group (>= 8 words) per world with its catalog counterpart by name overlap (training/results/knowledge-inventory-2026-11-13.md: 351 items, 313 with a counterpart); `tests/knowledge-inventory.test.mjs` fails on a NEW or bigger item against `tests/fixtures/knowledge-inventory-baseline.json` (shrinks with `--update-baseline` after a move). (2) One reading path: the only second reader of shards was `src/knowledge/layouts.mjs` (read `source.text(path)` itself); it now reads through `facts.wires` (new `world` option for the `core.word` wires of a world); no world code parses a shard (grep: none). (3) First paint: `scripts/build-bundled-subset.mjs <catalog>` generates `src/knowledge/bundled-values.mjs` (170 colour names with RGB, 222 number words) and `bundled-objects.mjs` (the 19 drawings of the 26 built-in kinds that k.objects has), and runs the two existing generators (act verbs, default face); `bundled-source.mjs` layers a source over it (the catalog answers when reachable; `facts.unreachable()` stays true while the subset answers) and the browser worker uses it. (4a) ONE reader, `src/knowledge/values.mjs` (`colourName`, `colourHex`, `basicColour`, `numberInfo`, ...), seeded from the bundle, replaced by k.values when the catalog is reachable (loaded lazily on the first read). Deleted: art.draw `BASE_COLORS` (29), `COLOR_ALIASES` (20), `VARY_COLORS`, the colour list of the help reply; data.charts `COLORS` (29) and its colour regexes (now built from the reader); docs.editor `COLORS` (10) and `COLOR_ALIAS` (9) (a highlight is any catalog colour mixed towards white); talk `COLOURS` (31) and `TINTS` (16); acts `COLOURS` (14); value-check `COLORS` (36); the formalizer's number tables UNITS/TEENS/TENS/ORD_UNITS (about 70 words; the grammar of a run is derived from the value; million and billion work now); the colour groups of art.draw, data.charts, docs.editor, talk and kids.shapes (141 words) and the acts-generated values lexicons (avatar, seating): all `extends=$lex.lang.colour`, which is generated from the catalog (`scripts/build-colour-lexicon.mjs`, stale-checked by tests/colour-reader.test.mjs). Webchat build now rewrites `../../src/` to `../../lib/` in world modules (worlds that import the knowledge tier did not resolve in the built tree).
**Bug found on the way (own commit 9b2d8320):** `facts.thing('legs')` on the online catalog ran out of memory: `drawnAs` was called with depth 0 from every nested lookup, so a kind-of cycle (legs, leg, legs) never ended. Depth is carried, a word is never its own kind, 4 kinds at most; test with a synthetic cycle. This also crashed the T1 and lookup runs of the "before" column until the fix was applied to it.

Done (general mechanisms, spec 21 section 9): one undo step per message (history cut per turn); `splitThings` for several things in one role; the matcher takes slot text from the words when a rewritten clause has backward offsets (the one-anaphora clause "draw a green house" had an empty thing: general engine defect); kinds from the catalog vocabulary at generation (`catalog=` on a kind; parts excluded); parts as kinds (`make.give`, `make.have`, the board's parseNP reads a part of the owner before a kind of the same name; unknown words go through the board's `open` path: "add a third window", "I don't know how to draw X yet", "3 different houses"); undo phrasing from `lang.undo` (new) / `lang.redo`; the world's own reader (act `own`, decline by the engine's reading, `kindWord` for `accepts`); `worlds/acts/matrix.mjs` generator (works, not enabled, see below).

| diagram pilot (771 ops) | 687 | 741 (96.1%; today 771); matrix 739 |
Diagram by cause: ask forms (check, path, extreme, cycle, members, neighbors, reachable, property), forget, swap, snap, flip, fit, respace, improve, untangle, straighten, resize, ungroup, rules, facts: closed by one mechanism (the own reader declines unless the engine names such a family and finds its targets) plus `domain.ask` for every question mode: +54. `it` / `everything` are no longer replaced by the last thing for layout commands (+7). Left (30): arrange of a named list (the clause cut), "show orange shapes right of Output", "Can you reach Done from Start?" (an expected refusal wording), "Nudge", redo wording of the discourse layer, rules whose clause is cut at the comma, the example scenes `ex.login` (connect after "and"), shaped-like and plain "to diagram" (art.draw-reads-diagram cases: the decline is not done for them).
Switch rule: diagram.sketch is within 5% of today on its tests (741 of 771, 96.1%) and its tab is not in the owner logs except 3 rows (diagram tab: one junk sentence read as a swap before the target check, now declined; one gain); art.draw is NOT (81% against 99.7%, owner log a 41 right against 45). No switch prepared: the diagram pilot's hooks still call the engine through text, and the pilot has no owner-log evidence beyond those rows; the reviewer should decide whether the diagram switch (its own commit, like ui.avatar) is wanted on 96%.
Matrix generator: `matrix=yes` in a schema writes matrix.sop, matrix.templates.sop and fns.mjs. Enabled on `plan.seating` it passed 7/7 in both reasoners and a hand conversation, but `test-world all` in the matrix reasoner then failed six cases of other worlds (kids.animals m03 / m06, time m09, user.profile m08, music x306, art.draw err02): the generic "undo that" says "OK, I took that back" because the seating instance is counted in other worlds' turns (ruled out: isView, undo=/redo= keys, the tally rules, adopting an unused world). Not enabled in any shipped world; open.
Side effects: tests/acts.test.mjs counts 15 acts (`own`); `worlds/art.draw/ops.mjs` parseNP and UNDRAWABLE export (real world, tests unchanged: art.draw 594/596 as before, to be confirmed in the final test-world all).

## 2026-11-13 hand-over packages and host boundary (worker; documentation and packaging only, no runtime change, no bridge deleted)
Wrote `docs/host-boundary.md` (every `src/` and `worlds/` directory, `worlds-pilot/` and the generator scripts: stays / lib / SKW with the reason; the turn pipeline by file and function; general lines per module) and `handover/<module>/` (README, copies of SOP and function code, generators, tests to carry, PROPOSAL.md in the target's questions format) for 10 packages.
What moves where: worlds/acts (+ executor, schema generators, coverage grid) -> std.acts; worlds/space (+ solver, pieces, layouts) -> std.space; worlds/session.things -> std.things; worlds/session.dialogue (+ src/dialog/dialogue.mjs) -> std.dialogue; meta.worlds rules + src/dialog/meta-worlds.mjs, symbolic-router.mjs -> std.route (cards stay generated here); src/knowledge catalog / facts / faces / learned + knowledge-lookup + loaders -> std.knowledge (the offline bundle generator exists only for drawable objects; the library needs the general one); turn pipeline pieces (budget, dry run, veto, all-or-nothing hold, tiered fill, graded fallback, why, value check) -> std.turn; failure-ledger format + grouper -> library tools; common.sense facts (336 lines) and the plan.seating / plan.timeline schemas -> sop-knowledge-worlds.
General lines still in PSys2 (SOP / code / generators): acts 458 / 1,117 / 580; space 293 / 1,648 / 28; session.things 263 / 460; session.dialogue 151 / 529; common.sense 565 / 62; meta.worlds 407 / 308; knowledge-loader 0 / 931 / 119; turn-pipeline about 900 general of 2,764; failure-ledger 160 generator lines; plan schemas 264. Total 2,401 SOP, about 6,000 code, 887 generator lines.
Notes: the turn-pipeline files are copied whole (they mix general and English parts); the exact general count is set by the extraction. No tests run (nothing executable changed). `scripts/build-world-cards.mjs` is named but not copied until the card type is agreed.
FOR THE REVIEWER TO FORWARD: sopworld worker: `handover/{acts,space,session.things,session.dialogue,meta.worlds,knowledge-loader,turn-pipeline,failure-ledger}/PROPOSAL.md` (Q-H1 to Q-H4, Q-H7 to Q-H10) and `docs/host-boundary.md` for its Round 24 host contract (the two must agree). sop-knowledge-worlds worker: `handover/common.sense/PROPOSAL.md` (Q-H5, Q-H6) and `handover/sop-knowledge-worlds/PROPOSAL.md` (Q-H11).
## 2026-11-13 (worker, MODEL ABLATION: every model switched on and off, end to end)

Report: `training/results/model-ablation-2026-11-13.md` + `.json`. Tools: `scripts/eval-ablation.mjs` (configurations x sets, sharded, through the heavy lock), `scripts/ablation-report.mjs` (tables, rescue classes), `scripts/lib/failure-cause.mjs` (the ledger's cause key, shared with `analyze-failures.mjs`). Sets: operational 1296, minimal pairs 122, knowledge 200, dialogues 2241 turns, wrong-tab 388, owner log a/b/c (69 with checks, 63, 33 without), ledger 573 rows by cause group, plus typos 995, loose words 670, MT paraphrases 146 and Romanian operational 2480. Reasoner matrix. Time per message from a single-thread sample of 400 rows; the full runs share 20 cores.

| configuration (all gold sets, n about 6,100) | right | wrong | asked | not understood | ms / message |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| rules only, symbolic routing (no model) | 4,978 | 589 | 183 | 334 | 38.9 |
| + prototypes (suspect-word leniency, "also" joiner) | 4,999 | 575 | 181 | 329 | same path |
| encoder: segmentation + prior, rules read | 5,038 | 601 | 150 | 296 | 165.2 |
| shipped (encoder + NX hybrid + T5 normalizer) | 5,073 | 595 | 140 | 280 | 185.1 |
| model first (NX neural) | 4,912 | 737 | 152 | 285 | 150.6 |

Per model (verdicts in the report): (1) model segmentation = the encoder's whole operational advantage (+19 of 1296; dialogues +7 of 2241; the rules' clause splitter + M1 are as good elsewhere); (2) routers A, B, C are within 3 messages of each other end to end (world level: A 70.5 %, B 70.6 %, C 81.3 %, encoder top 1 79.4 %); (3) the NX hybrid reader reads 10 of 6,744 turns, rescues 6 and breaks 9 (made-up-word probes), model first loses 126 right and adds 136 wrong; (4) the T5 normalizer +28 right (word-salad and typos), 9 broken, 97 MB; (5) rules-only Romanian 75.9 % against 78.0 % shipped (MT is needed for languages without rules packs); (6) the act tagger is not wired and has no kept checkpoint: 72 to 74 % agreement with the generated patterns.

Rescue classes of the encoder over rules only (140 gained, 62 lost): typos / near words about 67, multi-clause cuts about 18, other phrasing about 55. Prototypes (flags, off by default, spec 03 section 7.17): suspect-word leniency (a refused reading with a word one slip from a known word is spell-corrected: typos 821 to 835, wrong 103 to 89, no other set moves) and "also" as a clause joiner (+3 operational); together +21 right, -14 wrong.
Findings: `facts.thing('legs')` is unbounded on the online catalog checkout b705b3c (the ablation used the repository catalog); the NX gate and the normalizer break made-up-word probes; router A drifted (981 to 915 on operational). Library readers world-aware / READER-ROUTE add +4 to +15.
Test minutes: runs 11 configurations x 2 min plus latency sample 11 x 1.5 min plus 14 variants of 1 min; targeted tests only (runtime-config, dispatcher-runtime, encoder-dispatch, dispatch-validate: 50 pass; spell-partial has 4 failures that also fail at HEAD without my changes). No full suite.

## 2026-11-13 T2 taught facts at the knowledge tier (reviewer task)
Result (T2, both reasoners identical): other-view cells right 5 -> 9 (na 21, wrong 10); same view 16 -> 19 right (na 1, wrong 0). Causes by group: (A) the lookup in the view bypassed the overlay for "my X": the act reader strips "my" from the role, view.mjs `before` tested the role text only (fixed: reads the sentence); (B) kind-of not followed: overlay `ancestors` ignored catalog records (role `is=`, kind `is_a=`, `drawing=`), so sister/brother/uncle never reached guest (fixed, general); (C) the teach form read as a question: "ducks float" matched a kids.animals generated question pattern (priority 10) over session.things.bare (7); now a statement at 11 with a noun slot; (D) "my X is called N" is also captured by kids.animals as a pet (a private wire): the overlay reads it from session memory (works; the Talk answer says "pet (sister)", wrong wording, kids.animals keeps pets only for animals is a follow-up); (E) catalog gaps for the knowledge repo: friend has no entry; sister/brother/uncle/girl/boy/man/woman have `drawing=person` but no is-a person; waiter/king only worked because they were taught is-a; (F) remaining 9 cells: another world answers the tab verb ("put on a boat", "seat the dogs" drawn by the drawing world / shapes), fact-independent, owned by the acts verb claim (worlds/acts, not touched); 1 cell: friend (E). All 10 in training/eval/failures.jsonl with cause (exp-transfer.mjs --ledger now writes T2 rows). Files: src/knowledge/learned.mjs, worlds/session.things/{view.mjs,patterns.sop}, scripts/exp-transfer.mjs, specs/23. test-world all both reasoners 0 failures; things.test 11/11; smoke 62/62 after webchat rebuild.
## 2026-11-13 diagram switch (branch diagram-switch, worker)
worlds/diagram.sketch is the generated schema world (schema.sop + hooks.mjs + own.mjs; the engine wrapper is now engine.mjs, imports updated; the legend words of the old lexicon are kept as lexicon.extra.sop that the generator appends). Old patterns, operations, templates, fns.mjs, matrix.sop, matrix.templates.sop removed; reasoner=ops (no native matrix: the leftovers made six other worlds fail in the matrix reasoner). General mechanisms added: (1) frame forms `read.frame*` (non-tab, priority 30): a sentence that names the diagram or says "box/chart shaped like X" / "a shape in the form of X" is the diagram's whatever the tab, `accepts` returns 'focus', the drawing board never sees it; (2) connect after "and": the pronoun is replaced by the last thing only for the connect act, not for a whole "add ... and connect A to it" sentence (the engine reads it whole); (3) splitClauses keeps a comma after a rule frame ("from now on," "by default,") inside the sentence; (4) schema keys `tabUndo` (undo/redo forms tab only), `claim` (tabClaim on every form), `signal` (router signal), `boost` (priority bonus), `accepts` outside the tab declines unless the sentence names the diagram, a shape of it, named shapes or arrows; (5) `{x:words}` instead of rest for leading slots (rest runs to the end). tests/acts.test.mjs allows the role `post`.
Result: tests.sop of the old world 759 -> 758 of 771 in both reasoners after the final regeneration (today 771); the 13 rows that fail are in training/eval/failures.jsonl (class + cause, was_passing true): arrange/snap/group/align with name lists cut at commas (4), refusal wordings of the old world (ask_path.k02, group.k02), no-tab cases (create.k06, delete.k04, ex.star.n001), disconnect read as delete, "Nudge" read by kids.tasks, "from now on rectangles are tiny" without comma, undo wording. test-world all in both reasoners: 34 worlds, only those 13 rows fail. Node tests (diagram, arrows, acts, things, undo-redo) 84 pass; smoke 17.7 s green.

## 2026-11-13 sopworld 0.23.0 vendored; switch to shared things / taught tier / catalog: NOT done, why, and the plan (worker)
Vendored (commit 09caa09c): `src/vendor/sopworld/sopworld.mjs` 0.23.0 (build 2c7044be7436, 527910 bytes, sha256 in `src/vendor/LICENSES.md`, pinned at library commit 8b763ad), plus `tools/build-bundle.mjs` and the one module it imports (`src/knowledge.mjs`) byte for byte, so `node src/vendor/sopworld/tools/build-bundle.mjs <catalog dir> <out.json> <word|shard|world> ...` runs unmodified (probed on a copy of `knowledge/k.objects`: 2 files, loads into a World with `createCatalog` + `bundleReader`, `intoSync` ok). Vendor test updated, webchat rebuilt. Checks: `tests/reasoner.test.mjs` 54/54; `test-world all` 0 failures with `--reasoner ops` and `--reasoner matrix`. Lines deleted: 0.

Why the "thin adapters, then delete ours" step was not done: the library's pieces and ours are not the same shape, and the replacement is a rewrite of the consumers, not an adapter.
1. `createCatalog` / `bundleReader` TELL shards (schemas first) into a `World`; they return paths and violations. Our `src/knowledge/facts.mjs` (295 lines) returns JS records: `peek` / `thing` (senses, objects, commonsense, drawnAs, wires), `wires(type, {key, shard})`, `schema()`, `series()`, `faces()`, `countOf`, `unreachable()`, the retry-after-outage logic and the compute-budget ticks. About 25 consumers (`knowledge-lookup.mjs`, art.draw, avatar, talk, geo, the dialogue fill tiers, exp-transfer) read those records synchronously (`ctx.knowledge.peek`). Replacing it means answering the same questions from wires in a World (queries) and re-proving T1/T2/T3, Talk E-T1 and knowledge-lookup: a task of its own, and `facts.mjs` is being edited by KNOWLEDGE-OUT right now (merge conflict, and numbers that move for another reason).
2. `teach` / `forget` take a SOP wire and tell it at the tier `taught` in a World with `std.know`. Our `learn()` (12 lines) is only the store; the 140 lines of `learned.mjs` are the English forms (`parseTeach`) and the overlay (`withLearned`: senses, is-a chain to a drawing, owner things, `ancestors`, session-memory records). The English reading stays host glue either way. The overlay can go only when the consumers read facts + `taught` wires from a World, which is point 1 again.
3. Shared things / aspects / views / `show` live in ONE `World` whose things are made by acts (`make`). `worlds/session.things` is a registry over the items of tab worlds that run in our interpreter (`sync` of each tab's item list, `viewsOf` by the tabs' `view` operation, `adopt` per tab). Moving to the library's things means the tabs' items become wires of a std.schema world (art.draw, seating, timeline, docs, charts, geo all create their items in their own state today). That is the "tab worlds become schema worlds" migration (P1 pilot: ui.avatar and diagram.sketch in `worlds-pilot/`), not a swap of session.things.
Proposed order (smallest first, each measured on T1/T2/T3 + Talk E-T1 + knowledge-lookup before the next): (a) after KNOWLEDGE-OUT lands, one session World per session with `std.know`; `learn()` also tells the wire there with `teach` (both stores agree, a test compares them), then `withLearned` reads its wires from the World and the line store goes; (b) `facts.mjs` `wires()` / `schema()` / `series()` answered from catalog-told wires (`createCatalog.into`) while `thing()` keeps the record shape as a view over them; (c) tab worlds to schema worlds one by one (art.draw first: 7 own types, the selection / last-thing rules go to the library), `session.things.sync` shrinks to nothing as the last tab moves. Estimated deletion when all three are done: learned.mjs about 150 of 178, session.things ops/things/view about 400 of 462, facts.mjs wires/schema/series/loading about 150 of 295.

Next migration list from the library's reuse report (`docs/reuse-reports/psys2.md`, sopworld at 4938e83; 157 candidates, top 20: 8 believed): (1) the selection and last-thing rules repeated in art.draw (`ad.d.sel`, `ad.d.last`), diagram.sketch (`dk.d.last`), docs.editor (`ed.d.sel`), docs.reader: "a selection must name a thing that exists", one shared check (score 1.00); (2) orphan-reference denies (art.draw `ad.d.nokind`, data.charts `dc.d.orphan`, docs.editor `ed.d.none`) and no-two-with-the-same-name (`ad.d.twice`, `dk.d.name`, `dk.d.sid`: already `core.type unique=`); (3) the lookup actions of kids.animals (7: canFly/canSwim ...), kids.shapes (describeShape, colorOf), kids.time (monthDays): one generic `ask` act over knowledge (verdict: partly, not a shared module); (4) chess / tic-tac-toe `game` and `move` types (shape 0.80 / 0.75) as one game / move module; (5) `art.draw.tally` ~ `docs.editor.want`: the aggregate is shared, the meaning is not (partly). Reuse number 0 for most worlds (only chess 0.88, media.music 0.82, plan.seating 1.0 reuse from elsewhere).

## 2026-11-14 — Q9 defaults (switches on, clause cutter, routing note, facts.thing invariant, robustness ledger) and DEFAULTS BY THE NUMBERS / FEWER OPTIONS

All numbers: `scripts/eval-ablation.mjs`, rules-only unless stated, matrix reasoner, all gold sets together (n about 6,100), through the heavy lock; raw files `scratchpad/abl/d` and `d2`. Format right/n (wrong). Baseline = current main with the three switches explicitly off (`rules-off`). Note: on current main the knowledge set is 123/200 with everything off (it was 133 in the ablation report): ten made-up-word probes ("what is a glimbus") now answer "A has no common second name"; suspect-word leniency and READER-ROUTE fill both hide it, so the knowledge column of those rows is that regression being masked, not a gain of the switch (ledger material, not fixed here).

**Switched defaults (spec 03 7.17, RUNTIME_DEFAULTS, DialogSession, setProto):**

| switch | operational | knowledge | dialogues | typos | loose | para-rt | all right / wrong |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| all off | 1139 (119) | 123 (27) | 2091 (70) | 823/1003 (115) | 335 (166) | 22 (82) | 4983 / 649 |
| suspect-word leniency ON | 1139 | 133 (20) | 2090 | 830 (101) | 335 | 22 | 4999 / 628 |
| "also" joiner ON | 1142 (116) | 123 | 2090 | 825 (114) | 335 | 22 | 4987 / 645 |
| both ON | 1142 | 133 | 2092 | 840 (101) | 335 | 22 | 5014 / 627 |
| READER-ROUTE fill alone | 1139 | 133 (28) | 2090 | 823 (112) | 335 | 22 | 4992 / 647 |
| both + fill | 1142 | 131 | 2090 | 830 | 335 | 22 | 5000 / 625 |

Suspect and "also" are ON (`RUNTIME_DEFAULTS.suspectWords`, `DialogSession` default, `PROTO.also`). **READER-ROUTE fill stays OFF** (`reader: 'off'`): the condition "no regression above 1 on the dialogues" fails for the combination that ships (2092 to 2090) and it costs 10 typos on top (a typo'd "what is ..." question is answered "I don't know what a X is": "whats my nmae", "what is the smalles", "how many sides does a hexgaon have"); alone it only masks the knowledge regression above. `invariants.test.mjs` now accepts "zork" or its leniency spelling "fork" in the named part (nothing is executed either way).

**Card-driven clause cutter** (`cardCuts`, `readCut`, `registerClauseStarters` in `src/formalizer/rules/index.mjs`; starters = act / actshared / navact words of the meta.worlds cards, no list in code; used only when the uncut reading left something unread or one reading swallowed the cut, and only when every piece reads completely, in the swallowed case with a pattern at least as specific; pieces may be in different worlds; trace field `cut`):

| config | operational | knowledge | dialogues | typos | all right / wrong |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| rules, cutter off (`rules-nocut`) | 1142 (116) | 130 | 2090 | 834/999 | 5002 / 626 |
| rules, cutter on (`rules-C`) | **1150 (108)** | 133 | 2090 | 840/1003 | **5020 / 619** |
| DistilBERT segmentation (`enc-C`, cutter off) | 1157 | 133 | 2085 | 883 | 5061 / 663 |
| DistilBERT segmentation (`enc-C`, cutter on) | 1157 | 134 | 2086 | 883 | 5063 / 663 |
| rules splitter inside the encoder pipeline (`enc-C-segrules`) off / on | 1140 / 1150 | 123 / 133 | 2079 / 2078 | 868 / 867 | 5010 / 5028 |

The model's operational advantage over rule segmentation fell from 19 messages (report) to 7 (1157 against 1150); on dialogues the model is 8 turns better, on typos the segmentation pipeline's spell stage (not the cut) is the rest of its gain.

**Encoder gap (decision 3):** enc-C 5063 (663 wrong) against rules-C 5020 (619 wrong): +43 right of 6,100 = **0.7 point**, over the 0.5 limit; sets: typos +43 (4.3 points), paraphrases-rt +9 (6 points), operational +7, loose-words **-16 (2.5 points worse with the encoder, 216 wrong against 166)**, dialogues -4. The rule needs gap under 0.5 and no set worse by more than 1 point (here the rules-only side is worse by 4 points on typos, the encoder side worse by 2.5 points on loose words): not met, so the encoder stays on in the background (`helperModel` on) and never blocks; first load stays with the 68 MB model. Loose words is the group where it loses: ledger group "robustness: loose-words".

**Routing**: C with the encoder, B (the same setting, no prior available) while the model loads, is missing, failed or switched off: spec 20 and spec 06 say so, PROGRESS note under the bar says it ("until it is ready the world of each message is chosen by the rules and their reasons alone (routing B); when it is ready the model adds its hint (routing C)") and the model failure line says "the rules answer, with the symbolic routing B (no model hint)" (`docs/webchat/app.mjs`; the note API is `ui/progress.mjs`).

**facts.thing (F1, invariant)**: one `thing()` walk visits a lemma at most once and at most 12 lemmas (`MAX_WALK_STEPS`, `stats.walkCuts`, `stats.maxWalk`); `tests/facts-cycle.test.mjs` runs every word of `tests/fixtures/catalog-cycle` (180 kB: the real k.words senses of the online catalog b705b3c reachable from "legs" and "leg" by is_a, 119 words; the walks end with 3 cuts, longest walk 8 lemmas, 112 lookups) with a 5 s settle limit.

**Robustness failures to the ledger** (`scripts/ledger-robustness.mjs --result <ablation json>`, 596 rows appended from `rules-C`, class `robust:<set>:<cause>`, shown by `analyze-failures.mjs` as "robustness: set / cause"): typos 163 (not corrected 62, read as another command 59, corrected to another word 42); loose words 309 (not read 143, read as another intent 97, junk acted on 69); paraphrases-rt 124 (read as another reading 82, phrasing not read 42). No "unknown word" paraphrase rows: the reply text of those rows never says it (the 82 wrong readings are where the vocabulary hole shows).

**DEFAULTS BY THE NUMBERS (owner 2026-11-14):** (1) reading default = rules: `DEFAULT_NX_STRATEGY = 'rules'`, `nxStrategy` always rules in the webchat, hybrid and "model first" are no longer settings (the strategies stay in code for measurement); (2) the normalizer is off (`RUNTIME_DEFAULTS.normalizer false`, `MODES['encoder+n1']` turns it on, `DEFAULT_MODE = 'encoder'`), one optional switch "Help me with garbled sentences" (97 MB, +28 right of 6,100 = 0.5 point) loads it, never in the background by default; (3) encoder stays (see above); (4) options cut. ## 2026-11-13 P3 round 2: act tagger on real messages, segment first, `none` class (worker, experiment only)
Write-up and table: `training/results/act-tagger-p3-real.md`. Data: `scripts/gen-act-tagger-real.mjs` (owner logs + ledger + round-trip paraphrases, 1,095 English messages, 1,282 pieces = the engine's segments; labelled by the accepted readings of the schema worlds ui.avatar, diagram.sketch, plan.seating, plan.timeline, worlds-pilot/art.draw replayed in the row's tab and the five doing tabs; everything else `none`; held out: ui.avatar and diagram.sketch pieces and the robot / diagram `none` messages). Trainer: `act_tagger.py --real` (15 classes). 6 x 384 only, two seeds, plus a generated-only control. Result (seed 1 / seed 2): seen message agreement 78.0 / 79.6 %, act accuracy on accepted pieces 81 / 85 %, none precision / recall 95 / 80 %, span F1 65 / 70 %; held-out worlds message agreement 53.6 / 55.4 %, accepted-piece act accuracy 63 / 67 %, none precision / recall 79-83 / 55 %, span F1 59 / 60 %. Control without real data: accepted-piece act accuracy 60 / 59 %, no none. Much of the disagreement is the label (declined requests are none; rule slots are the span labels). Not wired; sizes 4 x 384 / DistilBERT not trained (the real sets cannot separate them). The grammar's new `own` act is left out of the tagger's classes. Test minutes: about 25 (replay 6 min, 3 trainings 6 min each in parallel pairs).
## 2026-11-13 periodic bug-fix batch (after KNOWLEDGE-OUT and the avatar/diagram switches)

Full run before: 51 tests failed. Files re-run one by one on current main; causes grouped, not phrases.

**Real bugs fixed at the root**
- A reading changed state: the executor's `referents` and `lastResort` (worlds/acts/acts.mjs) initialised the state of every schema world on a read, so every turn created ASTRA discussions for diagram, robot, seating and timeline (tests/astra-session). Reads no longer init; `_astraTurn` ignores a world whose state is only an empty object created on access.
- "What is a glimbus" answered "A has no common second name" (music enharmonic pattern with every word optional): `findPartialWindow` accepted the window "what is a" and put the noun in the ignored part. A window that ends in a determiner or preposition while words follow is never a reading (src/formalizer/partial.mjs, test in tests/spell-partial.test.mjs). Known leftover, ledgered: "what is a glimbus made of" is spelling-corrected to "climbs" and read by kids.tasks with "what" and "of" ignored.
- "My null is red now." (ui.avatar facelogic, a colour with no `of` matched an unset role).
- Duplicate link: the diagram's native refusal is back as an executor refusal: "That arrow already connects A and B." (acts executor connect and the diagram engine note), both reasoners.
- Fixture engine loaded test.seating beside test.counting (it answered counting questions, and imported the missing world acts): excluded in tests/helpers.mjs.
- Cards: the claim list of a generated schema world listed its whole vocabulary (meta-worlds size gate): capped at 40, words that are also action words first (scripts/build-world-cards.mjs).
- Stale builds: surprise pool filtered to the candidate examples (30 stale entries dropped, no 25-minute rebuild), cards and webchat rebuilt.

**What was built (the entry above is the first generated table).** `scripts/landing-table.mjs` (the table; `--log` appends it here, `--json`; also section "The measures that count" of `docs/system-map.html`, generated by `build-system-map.mjs`, so `tests/system-map.test.mjs` is its freshness test). `scripts/update-cases.mjs` + `scripts/lib/cases-corpus.mjs`: ONE case corpus `training/eval/cases.jsonl` in the sopworld ledger format (observations, append-only, an observation is appended only when a case is new or its outcome changed; `cases-runs.jsonl` lists the runs). It ingests failures.jsonl (2,148 rows, once, as wrong), the three owner logs replayed (165 rows: 45 right, 76 wrong, 44 unchecked), exp-transfer T1/T2/T3 (297 cells with the pair of worlds) and the coverage grid (2,572 cases, wrong = a hole); each wrong row has `group` (causeOf) and `stage` (new `stageOf` in scripts/lib/failure-cause.mjs: boundary / kind / world / act / things / missing / apply / reply / host). It PRINTS every right-to-wrong change in full (`--strict` exits 3); proven with a corpus copy in which two owner rows were flipped (both listed). Choice for failures.jsonl: it stays the file the producers append to and other scripts read (the failing view); the corpus ingests it on every run; `node scripts/update-cases.mjs --failing` prints the failing view of the corpus (2,504 cases). `docs/lessons.md` started (7 landings, library-relevant lines marked [LIB], knowledge repo [KNOW]). Per landing: `npm run landing` (or `node scripts/build-webchat.mjs --landing`) = update-cases + build-system-map --write + table; then `node scripts/landing-table.mjs --log`.
**Notes on the numbers.** The grid was re-run on this build (docs/webchat/coverage-grid.json refreshed, 1m47): diagram 15, seating 11, timeline 12, avatar 17 holes of 788 on the four schema worlds (the table also counts the pilot art.draw: 131 of 498, marked pilot). "Hand-written language lines" counts non-comment lines of non-generated patterns / lexicon / templates / operations / describe / matrix files, so the 45,979 includes big hand lexicons (docs.editor, media.music, geo.map, data.charts, docs.reader are the largest; diagram.sketch's `lexicon.extra.sop` is 2,523 lines of legend words). Pairs of worlds: a pair counts when one T1 or T2 cell is right in either direction (11 of 15 tried); T4-T6 do not exist as experiments. Cross-world on the ledger is a count of failing cross-world cause groups (routing, foreign-world reading), not a replay. The first run records the ledger rows as wrong and the replays as the current state, so 26 "wrong to right" are rows of the ledger that pass on this build (they were fixed since); there is nothing to compare for right-to-wrong yet.
**Test minutes:** about 10 (grid 1m47, update-cases 3 x 27 s, system map 15 s x 4, targeted tests 2 files). No full suite.

**Vendored 0.29.0** (library commit d1f8121, build 1f23ee172960, 561313 bytes; LICENSES entry, pins in `tests/reasoner.test.mjs` corrected: they still named 0.23.0 / 8b763ad). **Switched** (`worlds/acts/effects.mjs`, new, about 80 lines; `acts.mjs` +12 / -22 lines): every mutating act of a world with domain functions (the drawing board, the diagram engine, the face, the chart) is a `std.schema.effect` of one declared kind; `executor.group` and the parts of one act ("a moon, a star and a ghost") are one `act([...])`, all or nothing (one snapshot of the session state is the undo, restored in place); the difference of an act comes back as `diff`. Deleted: the hand-written group loop and the parts loop with their partial-failure returns. Bridge errors found: the library checks the required arguments of an act before an effect (placeholders), and reports a throw in an effect as an `error` refusal (the host rethrows its own errors and budget stops).

Cost: about 8% on a world's own test run (the difference reads `referents` twice per act).
**Not switched, and why:** replies (the engines write them; templates need a reply made from a diff), referents by words, history and the one undo step per message (the engine's clauses are separate requests), ask / pick / save / load / own (not effect acts), the generic executor's `run()` for plan.seating and plan.timeline. In specs/21 section 10, questions.md ("Q11 status") with four asks of the library, docs/lessons.md. New tests: `tests/acts.test.mjs` (all-or-nothing group, effects with difference, parts rolled back).
## 2026-11-14 smoke invariants after the art.draw switch (agent task "fix invariants first")
**Result: smoke exits 0.** Four invariant groups were red; three were real bugs of shared code, one was never the network.
- **Network (`network-never-changes-reading`):** not a network bug. The readings and replies with a rejected or hanging catalog were already equal to those of no catalog (the `deepEqual` passed); the failing assertion was `show Pariss` on the map tab answered Paris. The cause: the generated `pat.art.draw.ask.options.verb` (`show {thing:words}`) took any free words, and the draw `accepts` hook accepted every `ask options`, so the Drawing world read "show Pariss" as "list what I can draw" before the tab's typo repair. Fix in `worlds/art.draw/hooks.mjs`: an `ask options` reading is the drawing's only when its free words name the drawing (a kind of the catalog, a colour or shade word, "things", "options", "draw" ...). Spelling and the open-class lookups were not touched.
- **Destructive clause next to a clause that is not understood:** nothing ran, but the reply did not name the part that was read ("clear everything and zork the plimp" is one clause for the acts reader: the thing role is a coordination, `fork the plimp` has no referent, the world-aware veto fires). `heldPart` in `src/dialog/world-aware.mjs`: when the missing name follows a connector inside one clause, the reply adds `I did not act on "<the earlier part>" either, because I could not read all of your message.` The hold itself (one vetoed clause holds the readable ones) already applied; this is the same sentence for a coordination inside one clause.
- **Undo, one step per request (spec 15, owner rule 2026-11-13):** the acts executor collapsed every history entry of a turn into the first (`stepGroup` + `done`, spec 21 "one undo step per message"), and `add` of art.draw merged "and a tree" into the step of the first thing (`history.splice(-2, 1)`). Both removed: a request (one thing of a coordination, one wire of a message) is one history entry; only an explicit act group (`actGroup`, ids `grpN_`) stays one step. Two more gaps of the matrix path for a world that keeps its own history: `_redo` sent "redo" to the generic timeline whenever a matrix session existed (now the world's own redo answers unless the undo was a step of the timeline), and `_undoNamed` returned null when the timeline had steps but not the named one (now it falls through to the actions list, as without a timeline).
- **Tests changed:** `tests/undo-redo.test.mjs` "the timeline entries of a message share its turn" now uses the shop (a library world); the drawing is not on the matrix timeline since the switch. `tests/known-failures.json`: 11 entries removed (2 network, 7 undo-redo, 2 destructive clause) because they pass; the 2 left in smoke are the arbitration ones (`the world that answers is the world of the open tab`, ops and matrix: `remove the unicorn` on the robot and draw tabs goes to kids.tasks), owned by the arbitration task.
- **Measure:** smoke exits 0 (2 known, both arbitration); `undo-redo` 25/25, `network-never-changes-reading` 2/2, `invariants` 8 of 10 standalone (the 2 arbitration; "no message changes the tab" passes under the runner); test-world all failing rows: ops 62 (main 63), matrix 67 (main 71), no world got worse (geo.map back to complete).

## 2026-11-14 k.time version 2 by lookup, vendor sopworld 0.39.0, typed needs (agent task "Reviewer 2026-11-14 03:55")
**Vendored** sopworld 0.39.0 (4e88834, build df4bd5deeaf6, 590111 bytes): LICENSES entry and pin test updated; own commit.
**k.time v2.** `src/knowledge/time.mjs` (one reader: names + `core.word` facts, positions of one cycle, `next`, `season_north/south`, hour `part`; seeded from `src/knowledge/bundled-time.mjs`, generated by `scripts/build-bundled-subset.mjs <catalog> --time-only`, served by the bundled source; `loadTime(facts)` swaps in the catalog). The "cannot compare yet" branch of dialogue step 4c is replaced by `compareTimes`; `en.timeComparison` only cuts the sentence (before / after / during, hemisphere) and a phrase that is no time returns null so the turn goes on. Days, months, seasons, parts of the day, clock hours (3 pm, 15:30, 3 o'clock = am or pm said); month in a season by hemisphere (north answered, south mentioned); hour in a part of the day; two cycles are said not to be one. The 33 `timewords` of `en.local.mjs` are deleted (dawn / dusk are not in k.time: questions.md "k.time version 2 consumed"). Fixture catalog: only `k.time` updated to version 2 (other worlds of the catalog moved since the last fixture build; not touched). Holes: `during` h24 / h25 and the order cases h21-h23 have real expectations, 9 new cases h32-h40: temporal 14 of 14 right (corpus run 11: 1 wrong -> right, 0 right -> wrong, 9 new). `plan.timeline` gets `hooks.mjs` (`after`): a moment named by a time of the calendar takes `when` from its position and keeps `time: { cycle, kind }`; the tab sorts a cycle at a time; schema SCHEMA table 64 -> 65 in the inventory baseline.
**Typed needs.** ui.avatar, Talk and (after merging main) geo.map declare `std.schema.needs of= type= from=` in `world.sop`; `needsOf(world)` and `facts.load({ type, from })` replace the `needs()` hooks and the `wires:` / `faces` / `schema` / `series` strings of `preloadThings`; `validate.mjs` leaves `std.*` wires to the library; `src/cli.mjs` and `scripts/pilot-tests.mjs` ask `needsOf` instead of `ops.needs`. `listNeeds` of Talk stays (the type is the user's word). ## 2026-11-14 media.music switch (schema + acts; agent task "move media.music onto schema + acts")
**Result: SWITCHED** (rule: within 10% on own tests, owner logs identical, hand language removed). `worlds/media.music` is a schema world: `schema.sop` 184 lines (5 kinds: song, melody, note, chord, scale; 7 attributes: tempo, volume, instrument, key, loop, pitch, duration; acts make remove change pick ask undo redo), `hooks.mjs` 210 (roles -> the player's operations, decline hook), `reader.mjs` 180 (the music's own notation: note names, chord quality, scale type, interval, transposition, settings said about "it"), `songs.mjs` 15, `own.mjs` 8; the player, effects, sheet music and melody are `music.mjs` (the old `ops.mjs`, 2 exports added); `theory.mjs`, `abc.mjs`, the vendored tonal and the synth/renderer host are untouched. Shared frames: set `acts.media` (flag `playable`, 24 frames, additive, names `m.*`), first in `handover/lang.en/shards/frames-acts.sop` (own commit), after the merge with the w.lang.en-from-the-catalog change (which deleted the shard) in `src/lang/en.local.mjs` in the catalog format. Not modelled: kinds track (a word of song), instrument (an attribute: a word that is a kind and an attribute turns the generic forms into switches), playlist and beat (the engine has none); acts place, copy, send, save (nothing in the player to move, copy, send or save). Play and stop are modes of `pick` (the act grammar has no run act).
**Measure.** Own tests: 399 of 401 in ops and matrix (99.5%; the old world 400/401 ops, 412/413 matrix); 400 with the music tab open; the 12 matrix-only rows (m01-m12: refusal wording of the matrix rules, why-answers, assumptions said once) were removed with the matrix and are on the ledger as "matrix-only row removed".
