Ledger rows 14 (build `media-music-switch`); corpus run 10: 14 new cases, 0 right-to-wrong, 0 wrong-to-right. Owner logs identical (a 45 right / 10 wrong / 14 unchecked; b 63 and c 33 unchecked, as before; every reply the same row by row), T1 identical (204 cells, 97 / 0 / 107). `test-world all`: ops 80 failing (main 79), matrix 85 (main 84): the same rows as main plus the 2 above, minus n76 ("Write a melody: C D banana", captured by the editor on main, now the music's with an error reply). Node tests that mention the music world, compared with main: the same failures as main after the 7 fixes of mine (below). Smoke exit 0 (58 s). Coverage grid for media.music (new): 482 cells, 343 holes (the grid sentences use a thing "Zed" and attribute values of the schema; music is mostly unlisted forms such as "add a song Zed").
**Removed hand-written language:** patterns 3,276 lines / 377 patterns -> 1,597 / 171 hand patterns (143 theory, list, status and typed readings, 28 fallback idioms; 126 more are generated); lexicon 2,377 -> 1,293 (46 of 100 lexicons and phrase groups); describe 1,429 -> 804; operations 239 and templates 155 -> generated (248 and 163 lines, with 131 lines of extra templates for the kept operations); `matrix.sop` 128, `matrix.patterns.sop` 38, `matrix.operations.sop` 5, `matrix.templates.sop` 3, `fns.mjs` 240 gone. New: schema 184, hooks 210, reader 180, songs 15, own 8, 24 shared frames. Landing: hand-written language lines in tab worlds 32,000 -> 28,586 (media.music 6,910 -> 3,496), worlds on schema + acts 7 -> 8, grid holes 1,172 of 2,379 -> 1,747 of 3,211 (media.music 343 of 482 added), cases 7,691 -> 9,369.
**How the fallbacks were found (a general method).** The old patterns of the families the acts read were restored below the generated forms (priority minus 2), every test was run with the winning pattern of each segment recorded, and the patterns that never won were deleted: 236 restored fallbacks -> 28; the first, blunt cut (only tests.sop winners) broke 7 node tests of other files (bare "slower", "play B3", "Open the music tab instead", "play twinkle twinkle and draw a house", "Please play the queen to d5", "the G major one", bare "play" on the map tab), each fixed by a rule of the decline hook or a frame, not a per-sentence entry.
**What the node tests found that tests.sop did not:** (1) the discourse continues a request by replacing a TYPED slot of the last reading, so the typed chord and scale readings (2 patterns) had to stay for "and the G major one?"; (2) deleting patterns lowers the routing score of the world (words of the patterns), so the bare "play" needed one priority point over the game's; (3) a generated form has `tabPriority = priority + 8`, so once the dialogue focus is on the world the kept patterns need an explicit `tabPriority` (raising `priority` instead made the music read chess moves as intervals); (4) the matrix tests of the music (7 in `reasoner-chess-music.test.mjs`, 1 in `undo-redo.test.mjs`) tested matrix-only behaviour and were removed, `reasoner.test.mjs` lists no matrix music world, `music.test.mjs` names the 2 known failures; (5) `loop-guard` needs `budget.tick` in every loop of a world module.
**Lessons** appended to `docs/lessons.md` (what generalised, what did not and why, [LIB] a `run` act, a typed notation slot, unique frame ids; [KNOW] verbs of playing, a `media` aspect).
**Test minutes:** about 70 (test-world all 3 x 2 x 3 min, grid 3 x 3, landing 2, owner logs 3 x 15 s, smoke 3 x 1 min, Chrome matrix 2 x 4 min, two main comparisons of 8 min, about 60 small runs).

## 2026-11-14 geo.map ledger by mechanism (43 rows, build geo-map-switch) and the landing T2 rise

**Result.** geo.map own tests (matrix): 410 -> 452 of 454; ledger rows 41 of 43 closed (corpus: 41 WRONG -> RIGHT, 0 RIGHT -> WRONG via the new `scripts/replay-ledger-tests.mjs`); the two left need catalog words (Peking; the joiner `n`), listed in `handover/knowledge-out/catalog-lacks.md`. `test-world all`: matrix 127 -> 87 FAIL lines (geo.map 43 -> 2), ops 121 -> 82 (geo.map 2); the other worlds' failures are ledger rows that were there. `npm run test:smoke` exit 0. Landing T2 (taught facts): 14 wrong -> 7 wrong (before the geo.map switch: 9).

**Per group (mechanism, rows before -> after).**
1. *Cascade* (about 20 rows: n044-n050, n054-n057, r2n121, r2n135, r2n134 ...): one cause behind them, a coordination not read; no separate fix.
2. *Coordination not read, and read as many acts* (n043, n049, r2n015, r2n087, r2n121, r2n124, r2n126, r2n135, 9 rows -> 0): (a) the formalizer's yield rule asked the competitors in the list, among them readings their own world was about to decline, so the map's reading was thrown out for nobody's (`verdictOf` memoized, a declined competitor does not count); (b) the full-support rule split "Romania and its neighbours" at `and` (a conjunct that opens with a joiner `keep` word stays with the phrase); (c) a coordination of places is ONE act for show / pin / highlight: new hook `coordinates` (executor `coordinates`), and the formalizer neither prefers the clause reading nor cuts the swallowed command there.
3. *Sets of places named by a relation* (n048-n050, r2n094-r2n098, r2n243, r2n249, r2n253, 10 rows -> 0): one parser and one applier (`setPhrase`, `applySet`) over schema words; EU and other short region aliases come from the map's index (`peekIndex`).
4. *Sentence forms not read* (r2n186 km2, r2n231, r2n021, r2n022, r2n083, r2n087, r2n095, r2n134, r2n266, r2n289, n054, n059, 12 rows -> 0): attributes `location` and `distance` in the schema, 9 frames in `src/lang/en.local.mjs` (`view.default`, `layer.on.pin.verb|noun`, `layer.on.want.pin.marked`, `ask.speak.of`, `clear.*`, `ask.what.thing.is.in`, `ask.where.lead`; `turn off` for the off frames; `in_km_2`), first-person where -> locate, "the centre of the view" -> here, a route's endpoints taken from thing + place, demonyms with people / plural.
5. *Arbitration* (r2n075-r2n079, 5 rows -> 0): "the whole thing" is a reference with no name (like "everything"); the clear frames; a "map" word with no roles belongs to the map.
6. *View actions* (r2n041, r2n071, 2 rows -> 0): direction words are schema words of the zoom (`less=back`...), read before a place of the same spelling; "put the view back to normal" is a frame with mode `default` and the view's default is the whole world.
7. *Names the catalog lacks* (n024 Peking, r2n263/r2n266 demonyms, r2n125 "n"): hand-over list. Demonyms are read by the map's existing table (catalog-lacks.md).
8. *The "back" river captured for "inapoi"/"back"* (webchat matrix): a general rule that direction words (zoom attribute words) are not places; the vocabulary has no kind per key, asked of the catalog (catalog-lacks.md). The fixture catalog lists `back` for k.geo/north-america too, so the mechanism is tested without the network (probe "Pull back a bit").

**Landing T2 9 -> 14: cause found and fixed.** Five new wrong cells ("add a cottage / a cap / my dog / my cat / a boat" in the seating tab answered "I drew ..."): arbitration rule 6 of the geo.map switch ("a world yields to another world that has the noun as a kind") took `cottage` from the seating plan because the drawing has the kind too. The rule now applies only to the word another world calls itself by (its schema noun: map, document, chart ...). T2: 14 -> 7 wrong. Lesson in docs/lessons.md.

**Invariants** (the 6): smoke 0 fail; loop-guard (ticks added to `placesIn`); the stale-webchat test (rebuilt). Pre-existing, not mine: `tests/geo-places.test.mjs` "nothing is loaded before the host settles the needs" fails on origin/main (the bundled places make `buildIndex(facts)` non-null since ba245a8f), `reader-route` coverage (data.charts), 5 world-aware veto tests (known).

**Not done / hand-over.** The library `ask` / `std.schema.view` bridge (item 1 of the reviewer note) is not part of this task. Coordination words of the language (`n`, `&`, `plus`) belong in w.lang.en. ## 2026-11-14 live-state invariant (agent, worktree)
- Owner's avatar bug: the view drew a flash expression (smile / surprised after a reply, `transient`) over the expression held by the live world, so "already winking" was true of the world and false of the picture. Fix: the render model `shownExpression(face, transient)` (worlds/ui.avatar/facedraw.mjs) lets the world's expression win; the flash is dropped when a state with an expression arrives. Chrome probe: settled pictures after wink / sad / wink / happy / wink / wink agree with the state.

## 2026-11-14 BOOT-CACHE: the 5-minute "Loading the worlds", one manifest, a service worker, one popup (reviewer 05:30 items 1-4, 05:10 items 6-7)
**The 5-minute load (item 1).** Reproduced cold at 1 MB/s (page, engine worker and service worker all throttled) and with a stale cache of an older build (5181fac0 then the current one): the chat is usable after 3 to 13 s, but the panel said "Loading the worlds" for ever (110 s of probe: the label never changed, not even while the 65 MB reading model downloaded behind it). Cause: the engine worker's `boot-step` events (start and end of `worlds` and `reasoner`), added in db6781c1, had been lost when the worlds fetch moved into the worker; nothing ended those steps and the panel showed the first active step of the plan as long as the page lived. No loop on the unreachable catalog, no stale-index loop; the real waits are the 4.6 MB `worlds/index.json` (7 s at 1 MB/s) and the 65 MB model (70 to 90 s). Fix: the worker reports the steps again, the page ends whatever is left after the boot; tests: `tests/webchat-assets.test.mjs` (source check) and `scripts/test-webchat-boot.mjs` cold ("no start-up step stays open"). A user whose browser holds the old `index.html` (GitHub Pages `max-age=600`) keeps the old stuck page for up to ten minutes after the deploy; nothing in new code can reach that page.
**Policy (items 2, 3).** `build-info.json` is the manifest: `version` plus `assets` (a hash for each of the 364 files; modules keep the graph version their URL carries; `style.css` and `favicon.svg` links are stamped); `index.html` stamps the version. New `docs/webchat/sw.js` caches every asset forever under `path?v=hash`, shares concurrent fetches, deletes entries not in the manifest on every new manifest, revalidates only `index.html`, leaves `models/**` to their own sha256 caches. New `docs/webchat/ui/assets.mjs`: release reset (once per `CACHE_GENERATION` = 1: Cache Storage, service workers, IndexedDB, `psys2.webchat.progress`, `psys2.ortlog`; settings, history and every other key kept), the manifest fetched once with no cache, a stale page reloads once and says "A new version is available. Reloading once…" (session guard, no loop; the old banner only when still stale after one reload), the page posts the manifest to the service worker. Catalog shards stay cached by the catalog `version` (knowledge-cache.mjs, unchanged).
**One fetch (item 4).** The "early" fetches of index.html (worlds/index.json, dispatcher meta.json) were never read; the page now reads them and hands the worlds bytes to the worker (`worldsBytes`, also on a restart) and the meta object to `findDispatcherModel`; About reads the manifest of this start. Tests count at the server: no file twice per start, every request hashed except index.html, build-info.json, sw.js and `models/**`, a warm start sees only index.html, build-info.json, the models' meta.json and sw.js (5 requests, 33 KB), Cache Storage has one entry per path and every entry in the manifest, an update leaves exactly the new build (`scripts/test-webchat-boot.mjs` cold / warm / update / reset, `tests/webchat-boot.test.mjs`; `tests/webchat-assets.test.mjs` is in the smoke list).
**The popup (item 5).** `ui/progress.mjs`: the latest running step only in plain words ("Loading the reading model (12 of 65 MB)…"), no count, no "Ready", note and Details only in developer mode, a step shows only after 0.7 s (a start from the cache shows nothing), `quiet` steps (voices) never show, `immediate` for the update message; failures keep their notice with Dismiss.
**Romanian model errors (05:10 item 7).** Not reproducible on an English start: no Romanian file requested on a cold or a warm profile, with the fake browser Translator API or without. The page did fetch the registry and ask the browser's Translator about every registry pair (about 60 questions) at every start; English now loads the registry only (`init({ probe: false })`) and nothing else until a language is picked or Settings is opened. Romanian model requests (storage.googleapis.com, cross-origin) happen when the saved language is Romanian; an old saved "auto" migrates to the browser language. To see the failing file in the owner's browser: Settings > Language, and the Details fold in developer mode.
**Measures (1 MB/s on page, workers and service worker; headless Chrome; default settings, so the 65 MB reading model downloads; first 25 s counted at the server).** Cold, before / after: requests in the first 25 s 284 / 287, bytes 14.5 / 14.5 MB (the model streams; the whole start 97.5 MB both); chat usable 12.9 s / 13.2 s; the panel said "Loading the worlds" until the end / showed worlds (3.8 s), reasoner (8.3 s), face set, runtime, "Loading the reading model (n of 65 MB)" and was hidden at 103 s when the model was ready. Warm (everything cached): 5 requests, 15 KB / 6 requests, 33 KB (index.html and build-info.json revalidations, the models' meta.json, sw.js), chat usable 1.0 s / 1.2 to 2.7 s, panel stuck on "Loading the worlds" / no popup at all. Start-up table (step, ms, bytes) of the new cold start: page 2,532 ms 1.86 MB; worlds 5,319 ms 4.59 MB; reasoner 4,541 ms; model 89,272 ms 65 MB; translator 0; catalog failed (probe has no route to the catalog); voices 2,500 (quiet); map data 2,592; faces 6,033. Warm: page 366, worlds 472, reasoner 835, model 570, voices 2,500 (quiet), map data 315, faces 339. The size of a cold start did not change on purpose: lazy world loading and the compact index are the next task.
**Tests run:** `tests/webchat-assets.test.mjs` (7), `tests/webchat-progress-log.test.mjs`, `tests/webchat-build.test.mjs`, `tests/build-versioning.test.mjs`, `scripts/test-webchat-boot.mjs` (30/30), `scripts/test-webchat-version.mjs`, `scripts/test-webchat-phone.mjs` (all), `scripts/test-webchat-language.mjs` (55/55), `scripts/test-webchat-ui.mjs` (55/55), smoke. `tests/webchat-settings.test.mjs` "an explicit later choice of rules" fails on the unchanged tree too (settings.mjs not touched here). ## 2026-11-14 loading failures on the published phone ("Something did not load": Romanian model and picture catalog)
- **Reproduced** (headless Chrome, Pixel UA, 412x915 touch, saved language `ro`, published page): `Failed to fetch` for all six Romanian model files, console "blocked by CORS policy". Cause 1: Mozilla's Google bucket (`storage.googleapis.com/moz-fx-translations-data...`) returns `access-control-allow-origin` for `http://localhost:*` only (curl with `Origin:` of the Pages site, example.com: no header, also on the OPTIONS preflight). Every run from a real origin fails; our local tests ran on localhost and never saw it. The service worker is not involved (cross-origin requests are not intercepted). The Mozilla Remote Settings CDN has CORS `*` but answers 406 to a Chrome User-Agent: no way out there.
- Cause 2 (catalog): `app.mjs` calls `engineClient.call('catalog')` at the end of start-up, and `engine-worker.mjs` had no `catalog` op ("unknown op catalog"), so the step always failed: "The picture catalog could not be reached" on every start, with the catalog answering 200. Second, smaller cause: `index.json`/`vocab.json` (about 200 kB gzip each) had the same 6 s bound as a shard, too short for a 50 kB/s phone, and an unreachable catalog was never asked again.
- **Fix.** Romanian model files are mirrored under `docs/webchat/models-mt/ro/` (29 MB, `scripts/mirror-mt-models.mjs`, `mirror` per file in the registry) and read first from the page host, then from the bucket; each URL is tried twice; failed model downloads retry by themselves (4/15/45 s), then one plain popup with "Try again" (`progress.fail(..., { retry })`, `progress.resolve(id)` clears it). Worker op `catalog` (reads the vocabulary, `{ ok, bytes }`, `refresh` skips the backoff via `facts.refresh()`); the page retries it (4/12/30 s, then every minute) and clears the notice; bounds 25 s for index/vocab, 8 s for shards. Saved language Romanian on the owner's phone is expected (the model loads for `ro`; for `en` nothing is downloaded). Other languages are still bucket-only: they work on localhost only until mirrored (run the mirror script for them; about 25 to 100 MB each).
- Verified: `tests/webchat-loading-failures.test.mjs` (op-exists check over the page and the worker, registry mirror files match, download order and failure, refresh, resolve); local build on 127.0.0.1 (a CORS-blocked origin for the bucket) with mobile emulation, Fast 3G: all six files from `models-mt/`, progress 14 of 30 MB, no popup; unthrottled: no failure notice. `npm run test:smoke`: see the commit.

- The Mozilla bucket serves CORS only to localhost, so on the published page an on-device translation model must come from our own host. Owner (2026-11-14): keep only Romanian mirrored (docs/webchat/models-mt/ro, 29 MB); no other language is mirrored for now. Other languages work only where the browser has its built-in Translator (desktop Chrome); on phones they fall back to the rules translator. Follow-up for the next UI pass: a language without a mirror and without a browser Translator should say so plainly in Settings (not try a download that cannot succeed).
- There is no small multilingual model in the app (one model per language pair); a single multilingual model (NLLB/M2M100 class) is hundreds of MB.

## 2026-11-14 LAZY-LOAD: boot = shell + engine + the open tab's world; worlds, tab UIs, vendor assets and catalog vocabulary on demand (reviewer 05:10 items 1-5, 8; 05:30 item 5; 05:45)
**What boots, and why.** Page shell and 30 modules (it was 69: `nx.mjs` and `runtime-config.mjs` pulled the reading engine, `en.facts.generated.mjs` and `bundled-geo.mjs`, 560 KB, into the page for one function and one constant; now `nx-strategy.mjs`, `runtime-defaults.mjs`); `ui/tabs/meta.json` (id, label, world, order, effect names: no tab module to list the tabs); the engine worker and the reading engine (reading cannot start without them); the 20 KB routing index `worlds/index.json` (it was the 4.6 MB bundle); the bundles of the always-loaded worlds `core, lang.common, acts, kids.common, common.sense, meta.worlds, ui.shell, ui.help, session.dialogue, session.conversation, session.things` (English wording and grammar, the shell that opens tabs, routing cards, help: 180 KB gzip) and the OPEN TAB's world with its imports (Avatar now; the saved tab when there is one, `psys2.webchat.tab`, so its world is what boots, not the avatar's); `w.lang.en` core (the rules and the lemmatizer read it). A saved language other than English loads every world at start. Not at boot: the other tabs' modules and vendor assets (the open tab's only); the reading model and onnxruntime (idle CPU after first paint, setting on), the T5 (on request), the catalog vocabulary.
**Design as built.** `worlds/<id>/bundle.json` per world (hash in the index); `engine.reload(loader)` replaces the world set under the same engine object and the session continues from state and memory (the restart path); `makeTab` makes a tab's UI on first use (its world, its module, its vendor assets; the turn that opened it waits for it); tab effects register from `meta.json` and make the tab before they run; the popup says the step ("Loading the drawing world…", `world-load` events). A world is never unloaded in a session (the focus world and the worlds of the last turns stay). `?psys2lazy=0` loads every world at start (comparisons). Spec 06 "Lazy loading".
**Word index, both designs built; 606 messages (owner logs 47 English, operational 484 incl. long, Talk 9, catalog lookups 66); `scripts/eval-word-index.mjs` builds a world set from the index and compares it with the full set on reply, effects and state of every turn.** The rules' own pattern-prefilter keys are sound but let 24 of 34 worlds through per message: unusable. The index is the words of the worlds (patterns, phrase groups, lexicons, lemmas, joined names, tab names; 7,244 words); a word of more than T worlds says nothing, tab names are strong.
| design, T | index bytes | extra worlds loaded per message (of 34) | false positives per message | residual false negatives after the second reading | first readings not complete (read again with every world) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A Bloom 10 bits, T=2 | 13 KB gz at boot, no buckets | 6.4 | 4.0 | 13 (2.1 %) | 291 (48 %) |
| A Bloom, T=3 | same | 7.1 | 4.5 | 13 | 272 |
| B exact buckets, T=2 | 0 at boot, 3.4 KB per message, 56 KB per session (39 KB gz in all) | **2.5** | **1.0** | **10 (1.7 %)** | 317 (52 %) |
| B, T=3 | same | 3.4 | 1.5 | 12 | 289 |
| B + spelling candidates, T=2 | same, 126 messages needed candidates | 2.9 | 1.3 | 11 | 316 |
Without the second reading B/T=2 differs from the full set on 196 of 606 (32 %); with it on 10. The second reading: operational 220 of 484 (45 %), owner 35 of 47, Talk 7 of 9, catalog lookups 55 of 66 (a thing the offline catalog does not know). **Choice: B (exact), T=2, no spelling candidates** (a correction makes the reading "not complete", so the second reading covers typos; candidates added a false negative and 0.3 worlds). A's false positives are worlds times words (1 % of 34 filters over 20 words, about 7 spurious hits per message): 2.5 times more loaded; A stays built (`worlds/words.bloom.json`) for the catalog's 1.3 MB vocabulary. The residual false negatives are readings that look complete and differ ("give me an example command" answers "No. I do not use ChatGPT" instead of "Here is an example to try."; "Start a new page" and "stop dictation" in the UI test): adding the four big worlds to the always-loaded list did not remove them; "contested" and "runner-up" criteria for a second reading removed 2 of 10 at 65 % and 92 % second readings. Open: a sound index with slot requirements (a number slot needs a number), the router cards of unloaded worlds. "Did you mean" lists the loaded worlds' examples: it differs on 17 of 606 first readings, the second reading restores it.
**Catalog by the same index.** `scripts/build-catalog-vocab.mjs` cuts `vocab.json` into `vocab/<xx>.json` + `vocab/index.json`; `facts.mjs` reads only the buckets of a message's words when the catalog has them, the whole file otherwise (the published catalog has none: hand-over to the knowledge repo). Fixture catalog (6,422 lemmas), 348 messages (`scripts/eval-catalog-vocab.mjs`): whole vocab 42.6 KB gz; buckets 1.1 KB index + 60.9 KB over the 348 messages in 135 fetches (0.18 KB per message once cached; the first turns pay; on a vocabulary this small the total passes the whole file, for the real 1.3 MB it will not), 0 disagreements with the whole vocabulary. The start-up catalog step reads the catalog's index only (version-cached), not the vocabulary. The bundled subsets (`bundled-*.mjs`, 560 KB raw: colours, number words, animals, geo, time, arrangement) stay in the worker: their readers are synchronous inside a turn. Not done.
**Models.** The reading model and onnxruntime start after the first paint on an idle CPU (`idleCpu`: browser idle, no long task for a second, no turn; at most 20 s) and only with the encoder setting; the T5 only on request (setting) or the 30 s idle rule of before.

## 2026-11-14 LAZY-LOAD-2: residuals of the lazy-loading landing, fixed at the cause (undo after reload, 4 UI checks, the 1.7 % readings, the Romanian migration, the Settings switch)
**1. Undo after a reload (invariant: undo per request).** Cause: `engine.reload` makes a new session and the worker did `snaps.clear()`, so an undo of an earlier turn went through the page's state record (state and memory only). Fix: `DialogSession.portableSnapshot(snap)` turns each kept snapshot into plain data before the reload (the session is put back), `adoptSnapshot(rec)` makes a snapshot of the new session out of it (the old state laid over the new session's states, the memory with the undo timeline). Before: draw, a message that loads a world, undo of the drawing turn: the snapshot was gone. After: `tests/lazy-loading.test.mjs` "UNDO AFTER A RELOAD" (the page-level snapshot of the drawing turn takes the drawing back; the chat "undo" after "How many legs does a spider have?", which loads worlds, takes the drawing back).
**2. The four UI checks.** Three causes, none a missing await of the tab module. (a) Header line (desktop and phone): the avatar's head icon is a `span.p2-head-face` created at boot (its world is the open tab's), the test read the first `span`; the test now excludes it. (b) "give me an example command" and "Start a new page" (the die and the chips): the page set the open tab (`setWorldState ui.shell activeTab`) and the engine said "You are on the Charts tab": `restoreMemory` replays the matrix reasoner's timeline (`rs.restoreJSON`), which DERIVES a matrix world's state from instances that never heard of host patches, so every restore (a reload for a world, a restart) put the old tab, and any other host-set state, back. `restoreMemory` now keeps the world states in place across the replay. Also: `ensureRun` read the state BEFORE its awaits, so a click during the load was lost; it reads after the last await and builds the new session with no await between. (c) "Stop ends dictation" (same cause: the editor's mode state is host-set). Before: 4 checks failing (header phone and desktop, Stop phone and desktop; the chips as above). After: `--only ui1,doc,surprise`: header, Stop, "example command", tab switches all pass; what remains in the 50 clicks is the offline catalog (dolphins, "7 plus 5" colour and number words), the same on main in this sandbox (main `--only surprise`: 3 failing checks of 20 with different random chips). `--only settings` on main fails the 4 helper-model checks and the history-list height with or without this change (not mine).
**3. The 1.7 % readings that look complete and differ.** `scripts/eval-word-index.mjs --designs B --T 2 --nospell`, now 1,818 messages (the corpus grew), WI_DEBUG lists every difference with the worlds, effects and changed states. Before: 35 differences (1.9 %) among the readings not read again. Classes and fixes: (a) 24 of 35: an ACTION nobody had loaded (kids.common asks `prof.setName` of user.profile; "I want to play hangman" got "no operation for action 'hm.new'"; tic tac toe, twenty questions; talk asks `draw.add`): the interpreter records an unresolved action (`session.takeMissed()`), `index.actions` maps the actions of the worlds that are not always loaded to their world, the worker loads that world and reads the turn again. (b) 3: "what can you do", "what commands do you recognise": the help world lists the loaded worlds, so a help turn that called `ctx.worlds()` loads every world. (c) 8: a SWALLOW: a free-text slot of three words or more that holds a joiner or a question word ("what is the capital of Japan then how many days are in a week": the map world took the second request as the name of a thing; the clause cutter only cuts what the uncut reading left unread): `readingIncomplete` (in `src/worlds/word-index.mjs`, one function for the worker, the eval script and the test harness) calls it not complete. After: 6 differences (0.33 %): two are an artefact of the eval (a process-wide task id counter: `task_159` against `task_3`), four are one documented class: a loaded world and an unloaded one both read the whole message and the unloaded one wins by routing ("make it bigger twice", "go back", "and the day after that", "draw a nice chart about the current discussion"; no word names it; only loading it can tell). Cost: 996 of 1,818 messages are read a second time (919 before, +77; 55 % against 50 %), the extra loaded worlds per message 2.55 and 0.97 false positives are the index's, unchanged. Spec 06 "Lazy loading" says so.
**4. The Romanian mid-session difference.** Cause: the same as the missing initial states: `restoreStates` put the old, partial state IN PLACE of the new session's, so every world loaded by the reload (talk, user.profile, data.charts, ...) had no initial state; plus the tab/shell state above. A node probe (`ro-matrix-sessions.json`, rules translator, English start, switch to Romanian, reload at the first message, every turn compared on text and the whole state with a full start): 22 turns differed in state before (text equal); 0 after (the old state is laid over the new session's: `{...fresh, ...old}`; test "a world loaded later has its initial state"). The phone path through the machine-translation model cannot run here (the model is not available offline); the rules path is proved. A session that starts in Romanian still loads every world at start.
**5. Settings.** Developer > Experiments > "Load worlds when a message needs them" (`lazyWorlds`, default on, restarts the engine; the worker reads `m.settings.lazyWorlds` or the boot flag `?psys2lazy=0`), with its numbers and date (start 5.8 s against 13.3 s, 0.57 MB against 4.6 MB of worlds; replies the same).
**Tests run.** `tests/lazy-loading.test.mjs` 9 tests (4 new: undo after reload, initial state of a late world, state set during a load, the corpus of ten messages now includes the swallow, the hangman start and the help list); undo-redo, live-state, settings-coherent, discourse-memory, reasoner-restore (its "40-turn session" test fails on main too: a direct `rs.restoreJSON` call, not this path); UI as above. Smoke and `test-world all` in both reasoners: see the end of this entry.
`npm run test:smoke` exit 0 (82 tests, 18.8 s). `test-world all`: matrix 80 failing rows, ops 79 (the landing: 80 and 79; the same rows). Invariants green: undo per request (undo-redo 100 percent), live-state, network (smoke has no network attempt).

## 2026-11-14 12:30 — sopworld 0.54 vendored; avatar motion is a thin call of std.motion; phone popup above the input bar
1. **Vendor sopworld 0.54.0** (pin `919d20d`, build `b0e6cba3be8b`, 637493 bytes, sha256 in `src/vendor/LICENSES.md`): `sopworld.mjs`, `src/knowledge.mjs`, `LICENSE` copied byte for byte (scan-loops and build-bundle unchanged); `tests/reasoner.test.mjs` pin test updated; webchat rebuilt (`docs/webchat/lib/vendor`).
2. **`worlds/ui.avatar/motion.mjs`**: `createMotion({ lib, seed, has, reduced, t0 })` keeps a log of live events and calls `lib.motionPlan` (rules as `mo.*` facts from `rulesToFacts(MOTION_RULES_TEMPORARY)`: per-slot rules, priorities, `per=unit` talk stream with the mouth classes, `repeat` blinks, expression easing as steps); `frame(t)` and `nextChange(t)` read the plan. Same API as before (`emit`, `frame`, `nextChange`), deterministic under a seed, reduced motion drops blink and talk and keeps the first step of an expression, nothing is scheduled while the page is hidden (avatar.mjs: timer cleared, caught up on visibility), the library is loaded by avatar.mjs and passed in. Differences from the old scheduler: every mouth class lasts 100 ms (the plan's per-unit step has one duration), a double blink is its own rarer rule (two blinks can overlap), the blink phase restarts when the origin is rebased every 2 minutes. `MOTION_RULES_TEMPORARY` stays until `k.motion` is published. `motionRun` is not used: the host timer pulls frames (one timer, none while hidden).
3. **Phone popup**: it was placed 62 px above the bottom while the form + footer are 81 px, so the bar covered its last 19 px (the description). Now `placeAvatarPopup()` (app.mjs) sets `--avpop-bottom` from the form's measured top on open and resize; style.css: flex column popup, head fixed, body scrolls inside, face `min(100%, 28vh)` on phones. The probe (`scripts/probe-avatar-icon.mjs`) checks popup bottom <= form top and that the caption can be scrolled into view at 1440x900, 390x844 and 360x640: all PASS. ## 2026-11-14 LAZY-LOAD-3: the reading model on demand, one fetch of the shared modules (proved at the server), bundled-geo with the geo world (reviewer 12:25 items 1-3)
**1. The reading model and onnxruntime.** The first message does not need them: with `waitForModel: false` the rules answer until the model is ready (operational set: 95.4 % right with the rules alone, 98.6 % with dispatcher and normalizer, so the model is wanted for garbled sentences, not for the first greeting). Before: the model (65 MB) and then the wasm (14.5 MB) started on an idle CPU right after the first paint, in every start. Now `startModels()` in `docs/webchat/app.mjs` starts them (once) when (1) a reply is not read cleanly (no `supported` status, or an unparsed segment), (2) after `MODEL_IDLE_MS` = 30 s, (3) the helper-model switch is turned on, (4) Settings > Models "Load now" (as before); the step "Reading model" reads "loads when a message needs it" meanwhile. Helper-model semantics unchanged (off = rules only, on = used once loaded). Spec 06 updated.
**2. Fetched twice.** Measured at the server (not by summing CDP targets): on a first visit with no service worker (`?psys2nosw=1`, GitHub-Pages-like headers) `sopworld.mjs` and `en.facts.generated.mjs` reach the server once each; the page's avatar imports them after the engine worker's request filled the HTTP cache, and with the service worker they come from its cache. The "twice" is the page, worker and service-worker targets each reporting one request. The page does need both (the avatar's motion planner and `facelogic` read them), so no code change; the new scenario `first` counts each URL once on a first visit and fails if that changes.
**3. bundled-geo.mjs.** `open-words.mjs` no longer imports it; `worlds/geo.map/hooks.mjs` calls `provideBundledGeo(BUNDLED_GEO)` when the world's ops load (the only world with a `catalog=k.geo` lexicon), so the map's offline places work as before once the world is loaded (a reading that names a place loads the world through the word index or the second reading). `tests/open-words.test.mjs` pass; `tests/geo-places.test.mjs` "nothing is loaded before the host settles the needs" fails with and without this change (checked by reverting it): not caused here.
**Probe (Chrome, local server, 40 s at 1 MB/s cold and warm; 25 s unthrottled "reviewer-like", no catalog route).** Before (HEAD 75c07a96): unthrottled 25 s: 190 requests, 88.1 MB at the server (68 MB model, 14.8 MB wasm); throttled 40 s: 5.1 MB, the model 29 of 65 MB when the probe ended, the popup busy the whole time. After: unthrottled 25 s: 180 requests, 4.6 MB (no model, no wasm); throttled cold and warm 40 s: 4.6-4.8 MB (the idle trigger starts the model at about 36 s), ready at 5.5 s, `bundled-geo.mjs` not requested. The boot test `cold` (13/13), `lazy`, `warm` and the new `first` (7/7) pass; the cold test allows the `talk` bundle (the warm-up reads "hello" after the input is enabled; it was already fetched at boot before this change, which made the old check fail).

**Real fixes (by cause).**
1. *Ambient worlds* (the regression): `ambient=true` in a world manifest (`core.world.ambient`, `World.ambient`, ui.avatar). `createRouter`: an ambient world's reading with strictly less free text than the tab world's reading is tier 3 ahead of the tab's ("Put on a hat." was Talk's `send.view`, "Give yourself a hat." the drawing's `send.bare`); a tie, a game control or a tab command without free text still wins (routing "Start over", smoke "Save it as lunch" on the timeline). `given activeTab=robot` removed from the avatar tests (18/18 both reasoners on Talk); smoke's known loss "ui.avatar: What is the colour of hat?" passes.
2. *Undo / redo follow the timeline*: context carries `stepWorlds` (worlds of the newest effectful action) and `redoWorlds`; an undo / redo pattern of such a world is tier 2 on any tab unless the tab's world made the step. An undo/redo no longer puts its world in charge of the next sentence (`_noteWorlds`): Talk's "nothing to take back" had made Talk read the next question (kids.time scripted session, ops).
3. *Talk yields to complete readings*: `accepts` hook (spec 18): thing frames (look, parts, kind, isa, has, can) hold the focus, a send that names no place is declined (the face reads "put on glasses"), every other frame falls under the generic yield rule. This alone removed 14 failures (kids.time, arith "tell me how much", Romanian operational set, music theory, perf-fork fast path, phrase-log, spell-partial, ro-boundary).
4. *art.draw ask.count vs kids.animals* (no tab): a free `thing` that holds a question auxiliary ("legs does a zebra have") is declined outside the board's tab (hooks.mjs); the `given activeTab=robot` of kids.animals n38 removed; 46/46 both reasoners.
5. *Seating*: a guest's place is `{rel, ref}` now; the tab compared id / kind / n, so seated guests listed under "Not seated" (docs/webchat/ui/tabs/seating.mjs).
6. *Avatar decline*: the place of "what's in X" counts as a word of the reading and "what is in X" is not the avatar's name question (diagram `ask_members` rows).
7. *Dry run loads like a turn* (engine-worker.mjs `dry`): plan, load, second reading with every world; the voice dry run of "make a house" was never complete in a fresh page.
8. A tooltip for the avatar icon and its Close button equal their aria-labels.

- **Already done before this entry (found in the log):** grid v2 and the re-judging of the 146 paraphrases (85 same / 61 changed), the learning-curve tooling and the empty frozen held-out set, grid v3 generator (spec 25). Open items were only the re-measurement and the grouping of failures.

Honest reading: the grid is now easy. Acts of the cells: make 135, undo 91, copy 30, remove 23, change 20, ask 19; ui.avatar and docs.reader give no cells at all (no sampleable kind). The two holes are one cause (the library numbers a task list as a second list, the editor counts plain lists only). A `--per 120` run was started and stopped after 18 CPU minutes: the setup of a cell replays the whole walk before it, so cost grows with the square of `--per`; a wider grid needs the setup replayed once per world, not per cell (open).
- **Paraphrases, rules-C, same pipeline (`eval-ablation --configs rules-C --sets typos,loose-words,paraphrases-rt,paraphrases-rt-changed`, 22 s): paraphrases-rt (meaning kept) 14 of 85 (16.5%, was 12), paraphrases-rt-changed 3 of 61 (4.9%, was 3), typos 689/937 (73.5%), loose-words 240/629 (38.2%).** 71 failing meaning-kept rows grouped by cause (`training/eval/failures.jsonl`, class `paraphrase: ...`, 71 rows): other single paraphrases 25; animal-sound question in broken or compound word order 13; word problem with the currency changed by the translation 11; the question of a word problem not routed after its story (the story is read, the question answered "I cannot talk about that here") 10; music or chess notation in loose wording 9; arithmetic or time question in loose wording 3. No fix: the two largest general causes (the question of a story keeps the arithmetic focus; the sound-of relation read from knowledge in any word order) need a library or world mechanism with a spec; they are listed as the next work, not patched per sentence.
- **Learning curve (new point 66d79ae4, 39 mechanisms = 10 schema worlds + 15 acts + 14 verb groups):** paraphrases meaning kept 12/85 -> 14/85 (+2.4 pp over +1 mechanism); schema-alone world 31/60 -> 30/60 (re-judged by hand now, -1.7 pp; the seating world itself did not change, the drop is one request: "put Maria at table 1" with the guest name now taking "At Table 1"); held-out real messages: still a gap (empty frozen set, no new owner export arrived). Slope over the 4 points: flat on the schema-alone world (51.7, 51.7, 51.7, 50.0), slightly up on paraphrases (14.1, 14.1, 16.5): no evidence yet that adding mechanisms raises unseen formulations; the per-case sets (grid, owner logs) rose far more.

All five types compose (essay, scientific article, presentation, story, report); a text that claims nothing is declined ("I know nothing about X"); the result is memoised by seed and `docr.compose` derives its seed from the session and the request (the dry run and the real run write the same document).

(act templates are imperatives: there is no past tense in the language world; an act with no template that fits is counted).

## 2026-11-14 Proposer experiment on the 71 failing paraphrases (Q14 item 4; MEASUREMENT ONLY, nothing shipped, no src/ change)
- **What:** `scripts/exp-proposer.mjs` (prepare / cases / report), `training/psys2_train/proposer_experiment.py` (MiniLM-L6 raw mean-pooled and a TF-IDF control; torch and transformers of the training environment, no new requirement), new set `paraphrases-proposals` in `scripts/eval-ablation.mjs` (file via `--proposals`). Pool = 4,892 accepted sentences of the same world (clean operational rows read right by rules-C, tests.sop inputs, grid v3 right cells); slots (numbers, names) delexicalised and refilled; the normalizer's value checker (`checkValues` with the world vocabulary) plus a names guard; the rules read the first accepted proposal; judged as the paraphrase set. Results `training/eval/proposer/proposer-2026-11-14.json` + README.
- **Table (71 failing rows):** the ORIGINAL sentence reads right in 44 (wording gap, knowledge exists), fails in 27 (knowledge gap: animal sound 13/13, other single 13/25, story question 1/10). MiniLM first-accepted proposal: 14 right (19.7%, any of 5: 17), 10 accepted but read as another meaning, 11 accepted not read, 28 all rejected by the checker, 8 no proposal. TF-IDF control: 14 / 5 / 5 / 41 / 6. By class (MiniLM right of n): story question 6/10, other single 4/25, animal sound 0/13, currency 2/11, notation 0/9, arithmetic/time 2/3.
- **Findings:** (1) at most 62% of the gap is wording, at least 38% knowledge; a proposer with a raw encoder gets a third of the wording part. (2) The checker rejected a proposal the rules read right in 13 rows (currency word, number word counted as changed value) and cannot see a name swap (the vocabulary knows ana / mia / tom): a first run without the names guard "rescued" several story rows by an answer that did not depend on the name. (3) 10 accepted proposals change the meaning (notation letters are not bound as values: "G's major scale" read as "D major scale"). (4) Raw MiniLM is not better than TF-IDF at rank 1. A fine-tuned contrastive encoder was not tried (data principle: it would train on the same sentences; not worth compute before the shared reader work lands).
- **Not run:** `npm test` (a script and a data set only; `src/` untouched).
